# Blaze Camp engine — DECISIONS (Phase 0, v1.1; v1.2 review round in section D; round 3 / engine 0.4.0 in section E, stage B2 athletic work E-26 ... E-45, stage B3 selection / variation / rendering E-46 ... E-63, stage B4 integration E-64 ... E-80, fix round 1 / F1 time / volume / structure E-81 ... E-96, fix round 1 / F2 athletic work E-97 ... E-109, fix round 1 / F3 selection / dosing / the printed plan E-110 ... E-134, fix round 2 / F1 time / volume / structure E-135 ... E-159, fix round 2 / F2 athletic work E-160 ... E-177, fix round 2 / F3 selection / dosing / the printed plan E-178 ... E-205; v0.5 / engine 0.5.0 package V1 "structure and volume" in section G (G-01 ... G-24, with the list of converted / retired tests))

> **Status (2026-10-11, engine 0.5.0).** This file is a chronological log. Sections A to E record the decisions of engine 0.2 to 0.4. Where they speak of session minutes, the time check, schedule offers, cut orders, "fewer groups trained directly", the 7 % and 10 % step limits or the minimum climb, **section G (engine 0.5.0) replaces them** (COACH-CT CT-01; COACH-CX CX-01..04; round 4). The authority order is now: `coach_answers_round4.md` and CT-01 > CX-01..04 > `coach_answers_round3.md` > `coach_answers_round2.md` > `coach_answers_full.md`. The authority line below is the one the log started with.

Owner: architect (integration-step rows D-46 … D-66 by the integrator). Companion of `engine/CONTRACTS.md` (contracts/1.1.1). Every place where the sources are silent, or where a
source still conflicts with the coach's round-2 answers, is decided here **once**; code cites the id (`D-nn`) in a `why.ref`
or a comment. Authority: `coach_answers_round2.md` > `coach_answers_full.md` > `generator_spec_v1.json` 1.1.0 > synthesis docs.
Tags: **R2** = follows a round-2 answer; **R3** = uses the proposal of an open round-3 question (`round3_questions.json`, value
tagged `"r3"` in `model/rules.json`); **INFERRED** / **PROPOSED** = ours, pending his approval. Builders who need a new
decision append it to `engine/CONTRACT_REQUESTS.md`; the integrator moves accepted ones here.

## B2. Integration-step decisions (contracts/1.1.1, 2026-10-09)

Taken by the integrator while resolving `engine/CONTRACT_REQUESTS.md` and reading the 8 sample plans as the coach would. None of them
conflicts with a round-2 answer; where a builder's reading and round 2 could differ, round 2 was followed (noted per row).

| # | Gap / conflict | Default used | Tag |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-46 | A rep-max strength test landed on whatever station `layoutForWeek` marked (e.g. a 3RM on an alternating standing cable press for the swimmer) | strength rep-max tests (`tests.catalogue.strength.*`) sit only on **bilateral compound** picks: the mark moves to another station of the same bucket with a bilateral compound pick (the `work` lift first), else that pattern is not tested that week (the squat / hinge tests remain) | R2-PER-07 ("5RM / 3RM, 80-90 %" on main lifts); INFERRED |
| D-47 | Stairways that stop climbing in sets because sessions are full (06 / 07 from week 1-2, 01 / 08 late) were silent | `warning.stairway_time {week, muscles, advice, min}` per stairway block: target / own-sets muscles whose last-week T exceeds the placed sets by ≥ 1 because of `why.cut_time` shortfalls (muscles already in `warning.floor_schedule` excluded). Advice = longer sessions (last week's missing sets × `time_model.minutes_per_set.compound_bilateral` ÷ main days, rounded up to 5 min, capped at the longest allowed session) or one more day when that cap is not enough. The plan itself is unchanged (minutes × 1.10 stays the placement limit, CONTRACTS §4.5) | R2-SES-05, R2-VOL-06; INFERRED estimate; **round-3 question**: may full-body / L3 days place sets beyond minutes × 1.10 (12 v1.1 §5.1 used ~80 min)? |
| D-48 | Limited kits (field kit, KB only, bodyweight) could list the identical exercise twice on one day | a same-day repeat takes the next unused axis of the exercise's `variation_axes` (`detail`, flag `detail_variation`); when every axis is used the repeat stays (the V7 buy list names the kit that would add a real alternative). Added bonus / Focus slots never repeat an exercise of their day (`fillSlot` ctx `day_used`) | R2-LIB-01 (one-detail variation, never invented), R2-SES-05; INFERRED |
| D-49 | 16-week stairway, week 12: rules "2" vs 12 v1.1 table "1-2 RIR" (display level exactly 8.0) | **"2"**: the rules map level 8.0 to 2 RIR for every N (12-week week 9 is also 8.0 → 2); the 12 v1.1 16-week cell rounds the other way. Pinned by `tests/compile.test.mjs` | R2-PER-05; INFERRED (boundary) |
| D-50 | Event / competition date or gen-pop event inside the plan; lead-in shorter than 8 weeks | stairway to the week before the event week, `taper` in the event week, the plan ends at the event; an event < 4 stairway weeks away (or after the plan) leaves one stairway; a short lead-in is all pre-season with `taper_weeks_by_pre_len[W]` taper weeks | R2-PER-06; INFERRED (B2) |
| D-51 | In-season window ending inside the plan (no field for the end date) | `D4_season_phase.in_season_until` (ISO); in-season block to that date, then an off-season stairway when ≥ 4 weeks remain | R2-ATH-07; INFERRED (B2) |
| D-52 | 60+ default "landing drills only" vs catalogue `older_ok: false` on every landing record | select fills 60+ plyo stations (no opt-in) with landing-quality records that are `older_opt_in_ok` and not `high_impact`; V5 / H12 exempt landing quality from the `older_ok` clause. Catalogue values unchanged | C-11, R2-ATH-08; **coach to confirm** (or compile `older_ok: true` for low landing drills) |
| D-53 | V12 "bilateral before unilateral" and "work first" on days without a work lift / conjugate days | both apply only on days holding a `work` lift; strength-family conjugate days exempt from work-first | ORD-02, master 10.3; INFERRED |
| D-54 | V13 count cap vs D-34 / H18 | an over-cap or over-time day is legal with `warning.long_day` for that weekday; H18 (never 1 exercise per group) wins over the split-day count cap | R2-SES-05, D-34 |
| D-55 | MYO on a machine compound | allowed when it is the day's last resistance item (and the other MYO conditions hold); never on free-weight compounds | VOL-15 ("last machine or isolation exercise"), H02 |
| D-56 | V19 "jerk either" and dropped Olympic slots | a `jerk`-family lift fits a clean or snatch station (select uses it in the relaxed step, e.g. DB push press for a DB-only kit); V19 requires Olympic work only where some record passes every filter. 0 of 1,578 random / sweep intakes fail | R2-ATH-04; INFERRED |
| D-57 | Pre-season scaling "every slot × 0.6" | resistance slots × 0.6 (D-37); athletic and Olympic slots keep their sets in pre-season (the athletic budget's pre-season factors carry the change) and are halved (ceil) in taper / recovery | R2-PER-06; INFERRED (B2) |
| D-58 | Supersets in taper / recovery weeks ("remove superset") | light weeks drop every superset except pairs made only of small isolations (no added fatigue; keeps 90-min athlete recovery weeks under the cap of 12) | R2-VOL-07; INFERRED (B3) |
| D-59 | Filter 10 "drop the slot + 2 extra sets on the paired compound" leaves a group with 1 exercise | substitution chain instead: filtered pool → repeats in the bucket → same pattern other variant → no-ball / machine-isolation swaps → nearest pattern (two hops, buy list kept) → the same exercise with a one-detail variation → no candidate (buy list) | R2-SES-05 wins (2-4 per group); INFERRED (B3) |
| D-60 | Template gaps (one core station on `baseball_parts` days; rear-delt stations only from 45 / 75 min) | week.mjs tops the core block up to `core_block.exercises` on main days ≥ 45 min; when a week has no rear-delt station it re-adds the first rear station of a main day plus a second shoulders station if the group would stand at 1 | ATH-11, R2-SES-03, R2-VOL-04, R2-SES-05; INFERRED (B3) |
| D-61 | No cooldown bucket for the walk text item | bucket `WARMUP_GENERAL`, role / block `cooldown`, `text_key: note.walk_after`, no sets, outside the cap (no templates/2 change in Phase 0) | ATH-07; INFERRED |
| D-62 | Dose readings with no table | athletes' VOL3 "isolation = MAX" applied as a last-set MAX on small / rear-delt / calf work only; the core opener keeps RIR ≥ 2 in RIR-1 weeks; test items use max-intent effort; swings 10-15 reps, loaded jumps 3-5 reps at 10-20 % BW, strength-family brackets by role (work 3-5, accessory 6-8, else 8-12); a CMJ / drill test never carries "no max test from zero" (only rep-max / 80-90 % stand-ins do) | PER-11, H02, R2-SES-03, R2-PER-07; INFERRED (B3 + integration) |
| D-63 | Athletic budget (04 weekly targets) vs the doses the template slots produce | Phase 0 reports both (`athletic_budget.targets` / `planned`); doses come from the reps matrix per slot and are not scaled to the weekly targets (e.g. soccer 6 of 120 COD cuts planned in week 1). Reconciling them is a Phase 1 / round-3 item | R2-ATH-01; **coach to confirm** whether the 04 weekly numbers are targets the gym plan must hit **v1.2: resolved as D-73 - the weekly targets are doses the plan lands on.** |
| D-64 | Scope of `es_review.machine_translated` | the 866 lines B1 machine-translated; the architect word lists and 04 `name_es` lines are counted by source in `COMPILE_REPORT.json > i18n.es_by_source`. Every ES line stays PROPOSED until the coach (or a coach he trusts) approves it | R2-GEN-08 |
| D-65 | Compiled EN vs fixture EN (31 strings), catalogue `status` / `rule_refs` (61 records) | the compiled model wins for user-facing text (decoder notes removed, FIX-03 / FIX-04) and records the coach patches; the fixture stays the *shape* reference; the differences are listed in `COMPILE_REPORT.json > fixture_diff` | FIX-03, FIX-04, R2-LIB-07 |
| D-66 | Engine literals with no rules table (select weights and maps, body-weight tokens for MAX, the 90 % maintenance threshold, minor age 18 in `checkRelease`) | kept as commented `INFERRED` constants in Phase 0 (CONTRACTS §1.2 allows it); to move into `rules.selection` / `rules.effort.max` in Phase 1 | INFERRED |

## D. v1.2 review round (coach-eye reviews of the 8 samples, 2026-10-09)

The eight sample plans were graded D by a coach-eye review (151 issues; `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md`). Every fix lives in the engine
or in the amendment layer `tools/fixture/review-v12.mjs` (model 1.2.0, applied identically to the fixture and to `model/`).

| # | Question | Resolution in the engine | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-67 | A stairway the minutes cannot hold (time-bound plans were flat for 9-12 weeks) | `volume.sizeToCapacity`: (1) the structural minimum (every station at its minimum sets) fixes week 1's floor; (2) a priority-first probe of the peak week (its Olympic sets, WOD minutes and circuit dose) gives the room; (3) when the room is under `stairway.capacity.min_growth_share` of the start, block-level room cuts go first (1-set second calf, buy-out, repeated joint care, second plyo / ankle / throw station, extra stay-active Olympic piece, a non-priority duplicate isolation group on one day, third of a group, group frequency) - never the last rear-delt station, never a priority's group; (4) if nothing is left to cut, isolations enter at 1 set (`short_start_iso_sets`) and climb; (5) each muscle that misses its peak starts lower (never below the structure or, when it fits, the floor) so every week can climb; priorities lower too but fill first; own-sets muscles keep their start. The client sees `warning.start_lowered` and the schedule offers (`offer.lower_start` chosen, `offer.longer_sessions`, `offer.more_days`) before week 1 | R2-PER-03, R2-PER-04, UNI-09, R2-SES-05; PROPOSED |
| D-68 | Groups trained on every day when the minutes run out | step A: a group trained on two or more other main days leaves the over-time day; step B: the cheapest group moves to the optional day (never the work lift's pair - knee + pull / hip + push - and never the week's last rear-delt station unless it can move) | 05 rule_v1_1, ORD-06, R2-VOL-04; PROPOSED |
| D-69 | 2 x 45 min stay-active sessions running ~80 min | `cut_order.athletic_stay_active`: Olympic over budget, arm care to the optional day (knee / Achilles care stays on each leg day, D-32), the ankle block, one athletic piece a day (the medball throw stays when the profile wants throws), second jump / throw, COD, buy-out, then the common chain and group moves; the two core exercises alternate as a pair on any over-time day; under 60 min the work lift starts at 2 sets; sport tracks are protected for competitive athletes only | UNI-09, R2-SES-03, R2-ATH-04, D-32; PROPOSED |
| D-70 | Knee pain with deep squats, and a pain shift toward bilateral barbells | `deep_knee_flexion` tagged on deep / Zercher / goblet squats, Cossack and lateral lunges, deep split squats and the depth-drop family; the knee-pain share shift goes toward single-leg work (the coach's own Basic Instruction: "going unilateral ... helps") | VOL-03, UNI-02, coach instruction 4; R2 |
| D-71 | 2-4 exercises per group inside a timed circuit | V23 / H18 and the cut order's group minimum apply to full-body and split days, not to circuit days; the bootcamp cut order keeps one metabolic station a day and cuts the second calf and duplicate isolations first | R2-SES-05 read with PRG-03; PROPOSED |
| D-72 | Circuit stairway (replaces D-19's 5-s steps) | work seconds = start x the planned steps (3 % early, 5 % late), lambda-scaled so week N reaches `work_s_max`, rounded to 1 s and forced to rise every week while below the top (35 36 37 38 40 42 44 46 s over 8 weeks); H21 fails a flat or > 10 % circuit week | R2-PER-03, UNI-05; PROPOSED |
| D-73 | Athletic budget (resolves D-63) | the 04 weekly targets are the doses the plan lands on: tracks are ensured (throw, ankle block, COD, sprint), per-set doses come from `perSetFor` (target / the week's station sets, clamped to `athletic.reconcile`), Olympic exposures are trimmed to the budget and the profile's exposures (`trimOlympicExposures`), a week whose target for a key is 0 drops that key's stations and a small target trims them; V27 checks planned <= target x 1.25 + one set (Olympic <= 1.5 x or the exposures' minimum) | R2-ATH-01, ATH-01, ATH-06; coach to confirm the 04 numbers **v0.4 stage B2: per-set doses now come from the block-level athletic stairway (E-29); V27 also checks under / drops (E-44).** |
| D-74 | Validators that passed flat plans | H21 adds `stairway_flat` (a target muscle must end within 1 set of its week-N target or rise by >= 1 set in the second half), `total_flat` and, for circuits, `circuit_flat` / `circuit_step_over_ceiling`; a muscle named in `warning.start_lowered` / `stairway_time` / `floor_schedule` with a schedule offer is exempt (the D-33 contract); conjugate strength templates are exempt. New checks: V27 athletic budget (error), V28 realized bilateral share per exercise block (warn), V29 pull >= 0.8 x push unless pushing is a priority (warn - several non-sample templates are push-heavy); `assertModel` fails any unresolved `[PLACEHOLDER]` outside the legal release texts | review 04 major 11, R2-PER-03; PROPOSED |
| D-75 | "Tested from zero" | `rules.tests.from_zero_if`: level L1, a layoff, under one year of consistent training, or a minor (a youth baseline is never a 1RM) - not "no previous Blaze Camp cycle" (a 3-year athlete tests for real) | R2-PER-07; INFERRED |
| D-76 | Barbell hinge variants and the spine count | every barbell / hex-bar hinge, including staggered, kickstand and alternating deadlifts, is `spine_class: counted` (28 counted lifts) | R2-SES-02 |
| D-77 | MAX and intensifiers | MAX only on the day's last small-muscle (or machine isolation) item and only once the planned effort is 1-2 in the tank; the last-week intensifier never lands on a never-to-failure compound | VOL-13, VOL-15, R2-PER-05, H02 |
| D-78 | Class-model (CrossFit) day over its minutes | cut order: aerobic add-on, Focus, the common chain, then skill folds into the warm-up (no extra minutes), then the WOD holds 10 -> 15 min (`conditioning.wod.short_minutes`), then group moves | OT18, UNI-09; PROPOSED |
| D-79 | Stay-active rep zones | stay-active athletes' compounds 12-15 in exercise block 1, 8-12 after (`reps.stay_active_compound_by_block`) | PER-11, 04 rec_* rep_zone; PROPOSED |
| D-80 | Client-facing copy | warnings and schedule offers come right after the header; the planned effort drift is stated under the Basic Instruction; "Effort level this week: 8" (no "a 8.0"); day titles drop coach jargon (axial, clean / snatch day - except a CrossFit class's own lift names); non-competitive clients read a recreational profile name; equipment tokens by name; the printed plyo share is the planned one; an optional day that runs long because of its aerobic session gets its own warning | FIX-03, R2-GEN-02, R2-GEN-06 |
| D-81 | **Round-2 / rule conflicts noted in this round (for the coach)** - item (1) RETIRED by E-137 (fix round 2 / F1: `athletic.3` S1 / S2 are cross pattern; the same-pattern days are a variant) | (1) UNI-06 "opposite-pattern unilateral for athletes" vs ORD-06 "knee + pull / hip + push" pairs in `athletic.3` S1 / S2: switching the unilateral slot breaks the template's pairs (V11 / H06) and the 2-4-per-group rule, so the template stays as written (review 01 major 5 deferred). (2) R2-VOL-06 "nobody below 10 sets" vs the client's chosen minutes (2 x 45, 3 x 45 bootcamp, 4 x 60 class): the engine names the misses and offers a longer session / one more day instead of overrunning the minutes (D-33 / D-67). (3) R2-SES-05 "2-4 exercises per group" vs timed circuits: circuits exempt (D-71). (4) Credit-only arms for field athletes (D-03) vs R2-VOL-06: kept for competitive field athletes only, pending R3-VOL-01 | round 3 |

## E. Round 3 (engine 0.4.0, stage B1: time, volume and structure; 2026-10-09)

Source of authority: `coach_answers_round3.md` > round 2 > full > coach_answers > OBSERVED > INFERRED / SCIENCE / PROPOSED. Model
1.3.0 (`tools/fixture/round3.mjs`, applied by `build-fixture.mjs` and `compile-model.mjs`); engine 0.4.0. Regression tests:
`tests/review-r3.test.mjs` (named after the `samples/RECHECK_v0.3.md` sample + slug); review log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4.

| # | Question | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-01 | What does the stairway climb on? | 9 **lines**: quads, hamstrings, glutes, chest, back (lats / upper back), **shoulders (side + rear + front delts)**, biceps, triceps, calves (`muscles.lines`). Rear delts' own 4 / 6 / 8 sets retire; side / rear delts are reported for display and steer the shoulder isolation pick | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-01 ("I wouldn't categorize the rear delt as an individual muscle") |
| E-02 | What is a "muscle" for the floor of 10, the 2-4 rule and the time check? | the coach's **FOCUS groups**: quads, hips (glutes + hamstrings), back, chest, shoulders, biceps, triceps, calves (`muscles.groups`, core apart) | COACH-R3 R3-SES-01 |
| E-03 | How does a set count toward a line or a group? | group credit: a set counts at most 1.0 = min(1, sum of its member-muscle credits) (`timefit.lineCredits / groupCredits`) - an RDL (hamstrings 1 + glutes 0.25) is one hips set | PROPOSED (12 v1.2 §3.3) |
| E-04 | Which line carries a group's floor and climb? | `volume.floor.carriers`: hips = hamstrings, or glutes when glutes is a Focus line; a pain-reduced line's group is not floored (pain wins) | INFERRED (R2-VOL-06, R3-SES-01) |
| E-05 | Calves floor | one calf exercise x 3 sets per leg day reaches max(3 x leg days, min(10, 4 x leg days - 1)): 2 leg days 7, 3 leg days 10, 4 leg days 12 (`volume.calvesFloor`); a timed circuit: 3 x leg days | COACH-R3 R3-SES-01 (1 x 3 per leg day) + R2-VOL-06; numbers PROPOSED |
| E-06 | Focus muscle 1 / 2 | at most 2 Focus slots (`focus_muscles`, alias `priority_muscles`); each Focus line gets **+2 sets** on start and ceiling (`volume.focus.add_default`, range 1-3), bounded by the priority caps; the other lines keep their own steps (the v1 3 % cap on non-priority muscles and the +4 / +6 retire); `v_taper` takes both slots (back + shoulders) and no longer holds chest flat | COACH-R3 R3-SES-01 ("an extra set or two or three ... as much as everything else is balanced"); +2 PROPOSED |
| E-07 | Check-ins | retired: no `adapt()`, `adaptWith()`, `checkinRow()`, no check-in offers; `output_mode` is read and normalized to `plan_only` (`warning.checkin_retired` when an old intake asks for check-ins); `week_by_week` is the 12-week plan shown a week at a time (`plan.view = 'week'`); `previous_cycles.last_block_finished` replaces `last_had_holds_or_pain` (alias kept) - an unfinished block repeats the same start | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-02, R3-VOL-04, R3-PER-02 |
| E-08 | 60+ planned-step cap | retired (`stairway.limits.older_cap_retired`); 60+ clients follow the standard planned steps | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-04 |
| E-09 | Time model v1.3 | a set costs its work time plus the rest it is dosed with (`time_model.work_minutes`, `rest_class_minutes`): strength 4.5 min (work lifts of the class / strength families, heavy powerlifting-style main lifts, athletes' VOL3 main bilateral compounds), hypertrophy 1.5, isolation 1.25, Olympic 2.5, plyo / COD 1.25, sprint 2.5, core / joint care 0.75; a superset pair shares one rest (sets x (sum of work + the longest rest)); a rep-max test is a 10-min work-up, timed tests by kind; aerobic add-ons count as session time (`timefit.setCost`, `supersetMinutes`, `testMinutes`; `week.estimateMinutes`) | VOL-14 rest ranges INFERRED; minutes PROPOSED (RECHECK 07 'a 3-set work lift costed 8 min') |
| E-10 | The time check (`volume.timeCheck`, plan.time_check) | when the chosen days x minutes cannot hold the full plan (every group at its floor, 2-4 exercises, a climbing stairway): (1) tell the client up front (`warning.time_check`) and offer one more day (`offer.more_days` / `offer.more_days_min {days, min}`) and longer sessions (`offer.longer_sessions {min}`, the full plan's peak with every eligible pair supersetted, rounded up to 5 min, within the allowed minutes); (2) keep the time: room cuts that keep every group first (credit-only stations, aerobic add-on to its own session, WOD short / held, buyout, repeated joint care, second isolation, third exercise of a group, group frequency), then fewer groups trained directly in the keep order (Focus groups, the sport's priority groups, hips, quads, back, chest, shoulders, calves, triceps, biceps): a group stays only if every kept group still holds its floor and climbs; (3) supersets recommended (and turned on where needed); (4) every kept group climbs (`offer.keep_time` / `offer.keep_time_lower_peak`, chosen). The kept-time plan's starts are sized so the planned steps land on the peak the minutes hold (never above the level start, never below the floor) | COACH-R3 R3-SES-02 (order of the steps); keep order and sizing INFERRED |
| E-11 | A big mover that cannot hold 10 sets in the chosen minutes but holds the day's work lift | kept below the floor and still climbing, listed in `time_check.trained_below_floor {group, reason: work_lift_time}` - the only floor exemption (V22); kept only after the groups that hold their floor, and only if no kept big mover loses its floor or climb (small groups may give way and are tried again) | PROPOSED; flagged for the coach |
| E-12 | A day the structure overfills | a day whose fixed work alone (warm-up, athletic / power blocks, conditioning, core, joint care, aerobic add-on) fills its minutes holds no lift (its groups train on their other days); a day the kept groups' minimum structure overfills sheds the lowest keep-order group's stations (accessory first, work lift last); a group keeps 2-4 exercises on a day or leaves that day; calves stay on every leg day or leave the plan; the week keeps a rear-delt station while shoulders are trained | INFERRED from R3-SES-02 ("never squeeze 2-4 exercises per muscle into a day that can't hold them"), VOL-17, R2-VOL-04 |

After placement every trained group is verified (floor on the main days, climb by the last week); a group that fails first costs a competitor line one ceiling set (at most 3 times), then leaves the block (not trained directly) and the block is placed again | INFERRED (engineering), R3-VOL-04 |
| E-14 | Minimum climb | each trained group climbs at least 2 sets over a block (calves 1, a non-carrier line 1), 1 set in a block of 7 weeks or less (`stairway.min_climb`); half a set of rounding for quarter-set helper credit | PROPOSED (R3-SES-02 "never flat") |
| E-15 | Timed circuits (bootcamp) | circuits are held to the floor and the minutes: at the peak week (most rounds, longest work seconds) a day that runs over loses the lowest keep-order group's stations; a small group short of its floor leaves; a big group short of its floor takes a station from a group with room (`replaces`); the week keeps a rear-delt station; what the circuit cannot hold is listed (not trained directly) with the offers | INFERRED (R2-VOL-06, R3-SES-02, RECHECK 05) |
| E-16 | Maintenance / in-season / pre-season blocks over the minutes | the same trim (floor exempt blocks): the lowest keep-order group leaves until the days fit; warning + offers | INFERRED (R3-SES-02) |

| E-18 | Strength tests | a rep-max test is its own top-set item (role `test`, 1 set, no credit, `note.test_top_set`) before the lift's planned sets; it is not a stairway set; the time check plans without test minutes and a test-week day that runs longer says so (`warning.test_day_long {day, week, min, limit}`); a tested lift is locked before the laterality dial so the end test is the same lift | R2-PER-07; RECHECK 01 / 02 / 07 |

PROPOSED: the hybrid days also carry upper-body work only (pull / press pairs) so the legs stay fresh for the run and the floor of 10 can hold on 4 days; a template COND_AEROBIC station may name its colour | COACH-R3 R3-ATH-03 (approved); hybrid-day lifting, finisher dose PROPOSED |

V27 now passes on the whole random corpus (the v1.2 / B1 `todo` for fixed-rep throws is fixed: the station trim reads each station's own minimum dose) | RECHECK 01 / 03 majors ('V27 only checks for going over the budget') |

### E (stage B3): exercise selection, variation and rendering (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/render-b3.mjs`)

Regression tests: `tests/review-b3.test.mjs` (`R<sample>-<slug>`, `R3-<slug>`); review log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4 stage B3. Code:
`engine/select.mjs` (non-athletic picks), `engine/dose.mjs` (non-athletic doses), `engine/render.mjs`, `engine/explain.mjs`; model data in
the amendment layer `tools/fixture/render-b3.mjs` (rules, catalog tags, words), applied by both builds after `athletic-b2.mjs`.

| # | Question | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-46 | What may a block-to-block change be? (`- different grip` placeholders, A-B-A, days swapping exercises) | every kit first looks for a real library alternative that differs in **one detail** of the exercise anatomy (movement, implement, laterality, position, grip; two at most, each extra detail costs 3 score points): same pattern and credit class, never an Olympic-tagged lift in a strength slot, never a much more guided machine for L2 / L3 (constraint + 1 at most), never a home substitute from the real thing, never more than 3 points below the current pick (a barbell for an L1 / 50+ / pain client, a hot-spot lift), never an exercise the week already holds, never another exercise's version on the day, never an earlier block's pick of the slot (no A-B-A) or another day's pick of the previous block (exercises do not swap days). No alternative -> a **concrete detail** of `rules.selection.variations` (hand, grip, stance, implement, range: 'weight in the opposite hand', 'close grip (hands shoulder-width)', 'one leg at a time', '1-second pause at the bottom' ...; `detail = {axis, key: detail.v.<id>, value}`); no concrete value -> the exercise stays and the day's next slot takes the change. Every kit changes at least one slot a day; a kept exercise keeps its block's detail | COACH-R2 R2-LIB-01 ('show it as "Reverse lunge - dumbbell in opposite hand"'), COACH-ANSWER PER-02 ('one or two of grip, stance, implement, range ... never all at once'); vocabulary and scoring PROPOSED |
| E-47 | Near-duplicates in one session | the same movement, implement and credit class differing only in trivial position / grip words (seated vs bent-over reverse fly, DB floor press vs alternating DB floor press, two pulldown grips) is **one exercise**: a session never holds two (relaxed only when nothing else passes the kit); a second weekly exposure of another version is penalised (-2) | COACH-R2 R2-SES-05 ('2-4 different exercises'), PER-04; RECHECK 01 / 03 majors; INFERRED |
| E-48 | A second exposure the kit cannot vary | when the week's only real option is an exercise already used, or the alternatives are home substitutes / a one-arm lead press, the real exercise comes back with one concrete detail ('Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time') and keeps it in the next block | PER-04, R2-LIB-01, R2-LIB-07; RECHECK 02 / 06 minors |
| E-49 | Lead lifts | the day's first chest press is a two-arm press when the kit holds one (alternating / one-arm -6), never a floor press or push-up while a bench press passes (-4); heavy-powerlifting clients press with both arms (-4 otherwise); the axial work lift of class-model / strength / physique clients is a canonical barbell lift (front or high-bar back squat; conventional, sumo or hex-bar deadlift; +4); L1, 50+ and pain clients take dumbbells / kettlebells / machines before a barbell (-4); an L2 / L3 Focus for a big muscle takes load (body-weight -3) | ORD-07, VOL-12, UNI-02, EQ-03, EQ-09; RECHECK 03 / 04 / 05 / 06 / 07 / 08; weights INFERRED |
| E-50 | Records that name no implement or two exercises | swap-only (`selection.generic_id_patterns` + `-either`, `any-variation`, the inferred barbell alternating deadlift, `seated-or-lying`, `-verify`, `typo-ambiguous`); the box-squat name loses 'Deload' | FIX-03 / FIX-04, R2-LIB-01; RECHECK 01 / 06 / 08 |
| E-51 | Home substitutes | only when no loaded or machine version of the slot passes the kit (`selection.home_substitute_only_when_needed`), never at a block change from the real exercise | R2-LIB-07 ('for home clients'); RECHECK 02 / 03 / 05 / 06 / 07 |
| E-52 | Core, skill and joint-care picks | every core exercise once a week (main and optional days); core swaps in the pick's plane; the low-back-rotation hot spot avoids loaded flexion-rotation (Russian twist, sit-ups, V-ups, wood chops) and prefers anti-rotation; knee pain avoids the long-lever Copenhagen plank; the class model's skill station rotates the coach's program skills (double-unders, jump rope, hollow rocks) and follows the template's note ('DUBS'); the racket elbow takes the catalog's wrist extension (`db-wrist-extension-slow-lowering`); the lunge / split-squat family and full-catch Olympic lifts are deep knee bends (knee pain filters picks and swaps); swaps at or below the client's ladder come first | CAT-08, GEN-04, profile core_rules, D-23; RECHECK 02 / 04 / 08; INFERRED |
| E-53 | Intensifiers | only in the last stairway week, **one per training day**: a MYO set on the day's last resistance item when it is a small-muscle or machine isolation exercise; never a MAX set (always inside 1-3 RIR), never a glute bridge / thrust or a free-weight compound, never calves for an Achilles hot spot; `effort.intensifier` only on that item; the copy names the week (`effort.drift_intensifier`) and the week header prints it only when an item carries one | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-04 ('to 1 + intensifiers in the last week, always inside your 1-3'); VOL-15; one a day PROPOSED; RECHECK 01 / 02 / 04 / 06 / 07 / 08 |
| E-54 | Circuit effort | a resistance circuit station follows the planned RIR of the stairway week ('about {rir} reps left at the bell'; `effort {type: rir, circuit: true, key}`); a body-weight station picks a version or tempo (no week-1 'weight' tip); a single-side station switches sides at half time | R2-PER-05, COACH-R3 R3-VOL-04; RECHECK 05 major / minors |
| E-55 | Rep scheme stability | the 12/10/8 -20 pyramid is decided for the whole exercise block (the slot's sets in the block's first week, `doseItem input.block_first_sets`) and never for heavy-powerlifting clients; a supersetted work lift keeps its 3-6 min rest after the pair (`rest {type: after_pair, min, max}`); fixed class-model reps meet the exercise's own ceiling (weighted pull-up 6-8) | VOL-12, VOL-14, PER-02; RECHECK 02 / 04 / 07 |
| E-56 | Rep phase and the season | the athlete's VOL phase follows the season phase of the exercise block's first week (`reps.athlete_vol_phases.by_season_phase`: OFF_LATE starts at VOL2); shape.mjs minimal edit | PER-11; RECHECK 03 major |
| E-57 | Aerobic sessions | named by the client's own cardio tool (`D8_kit.cardio_options`, intake.mjs additive), the colour taking its own tool from the list, low-impact tools only for lower-limb pain when one exists; the coach's colour record gives the pattern (work / easy times, steady, short sprints) written for that tool ('3 min hard / 3 min easy', '8 min steady / 1 min easy', '20 s run / 40 s walk'); the record climbs the colour ladder from the client's level (L1 / L2 / L3 start 0 / 1 / 2) one step per exercise block; an aerobic test is written on the card | EQ-14 ('any erg'), ATH-07, R2-SES-06; ladder / pattern PROPOSED; RECHECK 03 / 04 majors, 07 / 08 minors |
| E-58 | WOD stations | a load per loaded station by level (dumbbells per hand, the kettlebell; `wod.station_loads`), the format's rotation words, a calorie station written once ('15 cal on the bike, rower or SkiErg') | PRG-08 / PRG-10; loads PROPOSED; RECHECK 04 major |
| E-59 | The printed plan | the use-at-your-own-risk line with the release link on top of every plan (EN / ES `rules.legal.disclaimer_url`; a short line on every view and fragment, refer and invalid plans too); 'Your schedule' from `plan.time_check` (trained directly, not trained directly because of time / through compound lifts / no station, held below the floor, the offers in numbers); each day: the blocks that can be switched off, the supersets that save time (unless `prefs.superset_recommendations: hide`), 'supersets marked today' when needed, superset pair tags `[A1] [A2]`; macro views for screenshots (`view: 'overview'`; stairway table week · level · effort · sets / work seconds; weekly sets per FOCUS group; the week at a glance; one table per day); test items once ('Test: countermovement jump'), the end-test header by test type, no test rows in the block table; failed checks in the client's words (`check.failed.<id>`); a day title rebuilt from the day's own lifts when the template names a lift it no longer holds; a Christmas / New Year notice; the coach's cardio line for sport-led cardio when the plan has no aerobic session | COACH-R3 R3-GEN-01, R3-SES-02, R3-SES-01; R2-GEN-02; RECHECK 01-08 minors |
| E-60 | Check-in words | retired from the model: `felt.*`, `intake.error.week_by_week_needs_checkin`; `effort.ten` loses 'mark that exercise'; `why.pro_setting` / `why.no_checkin_planned` lose 'check-in'; the stair level reads 'Week level: n of 11' (never 'Effort level' next to an RIR) | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-02 ('I want to take away all check-in info'); RECHECK 02 minor |
| E-61 | Spanish | a generic pull is a 'tirón' (the 'jalón' is the lat pulldown): Olympic pulls, day labels, PPL; 'aperturas posteriores' for reverse flies; weekdays inside a sentence in lower case; '1 año'; the capacity and season notices reworded; English gym term in brackets kept | COACH-R2 R2-GEN-08; RECHECK 03 minor; wording PROPOSED (coach ES review, D-64) |
| E-62 | Coach-voice cues | coach-to-coach notes and kit-specific clauses leave the client's cue ('the client feels the right muscles', '(or a barbell)' on a dumbbell lift) | GEN-07; RECHECK 02 / 03 / 05 minors |

### E (stage B4): integration (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/integrate-b4.mjs`, `tools/sweep.mjs`)

Stage B4 ran the whole pipeline from clean (fixture, compile, check-model, tests, the 8 samples) and a sweep of 1,500 seeded random
intakes + every picker sport (52) + every activity (26) on the fixture and on `model/` (`node tools/sweep.mjs 1500 --render`, EN + ES
rendered). Before B4: 15 of 1,578 intakes failed an error check on each model (V27 x 8, V19 x 3, H21 x 2, V23 / H18 x 2, V14 / H08 x 1);
after B4: 0 on both. The 8 regenerated plans were read end to end. Regression tests: `tests/review-b4.test.mjs` (`R<sample>-<slug>` for
an issue found in a sample plan, `B4-sweep-random-<n>-<slug>` for a sweep intake, index n of `tests/helpers/random-intake.mjs`); review
log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4 stage B4. Model data in the amendment layer `tools/fixture/integrate-b4.mjs`, applied by both builds
after `render-b3.mjs`.

| # | Question | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-64 | A class-model day named for a lift the time check removed ('Squat + clean day' with no squat, 'Deadlift day' with no deadlift; sample 04) | in the class model (`rules.session.time_fit.keep_work_lift`: family `hybrid_crossfit`, role `work`) the day's work lift is the class's strength piece and the time check never removes it. A group the time check does not train directly keeps that one lift at the class table's 3 sets (3 x 7 -> 3 x 4, heavier by the week; no set stairway, no trims) and is listed `not_trained_directly {group, reason: 'class_lift'}`; it is fixed structure in the minutes (like the Olympic piece), it leaves a day whose fixed class work cannot hold it (a 30-minute class keeps the v0.4 behaviour). Validators: V22 / H17 do not read a kept class lift as 'listed but trained'; V23 / H18 accept the class model's work lift alone in its group on a day; V18 does not make a leg day of a kept lower class lift; H21's main-day-total rule skips a block that trains no group directly (all class lifts). Render: 'Your class lifts stay for these groups (3 sets, fewer reps and more weight as the weeks go; no extra sets)'. Sample 04 now: squat + clean pull Monday, strict press + bench Tuesday, deadlift + snatch pull Thursday, row + pull-ups Friday; back and chest trained directly | COACH-R3 R3-SES-02 (his example: 'a CrossFit class member with 4 x 60 min'; 'big movers first'), OBSERVED OT18 HALF TIME table; keeping the lift PROPOSED (the B1 open question 'keep the class's squat / deadlift as flat work sets outside the stairway') |

### E (fix round 1 / F1): time, volume and structure (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/fix-r1-f1.mjs`)

Fix round 1 answered the coach-eye reviews of the 8 sample plans (grades C- ... D) in the time / volume / structure area
(`engine/volume.mjs`, `week.mjs`, `generate.mjs`, `validate.mjs`, `shape.mjs`). Model data in the amendment layer
`tools/fixture/fix-r1-f1.mjs` (`amendRulesF1`, `UI_F1`), applied by both builds after `integrate-b4.mjs` (`build-fixture.mjs`,
`compile-model.mjs`, `tools/compile/i18n.mjs` - one import and one call each). Regression tests: `tests/review-f1.test.mjs`
(`R<sample>-<slug>` per review issue, `F1-sweep-<intake>-<slug>` for a sweep intake the round's own changes broke on the way); review log:
`samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4 fix round 1. Every value is PROPOSED unless the row says otherwise.

| # | Question | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-81 | The time check kept the cheap shoulder isolation and dropped the swimmer's quads (01 blocker); the class kept back and chest with bodybuilding accessories while quads, hips and shoulders went (04 blocker) | the keep order is the client's Focus groups, then the sport's big priority groups, then the big movers (`keep_order` big: quads, hips, back, chest), then the sport's small priorities, then the rest (`volume.keepOrder`; a group is 'big' when one of its lines is). A big mover that fails its probe is retried before any small group is admitted: (1) relaxed - kept below its floor with its work lift heavier by the week (E-11, `trained_below_floor` reason `work_lift_time`), (2) with the stations of the small groups on its contested days shed for that probe (their slots become room cuts). When a day must shed a group, the group with stations on 2+ other days and no work role goes first. Sample 01 now trains quads, hips, back; 04 quads and back (below the floor, the class lifts heavier by the week), hips and shoulders keep their class lifts, chest goes | COACH-R3 R3-SES-02 ('big movers first; arms, calves and shoulder isolation come from compound credit'); 04_profiles swimming priorities (INFERRED) |
| E-82 | A week-1 set that fits the week-1 minutes made the whole day fail at the peak (01: quads lost to a 1-minute overrun on Monday at week 12) | while the time check sizes week 1, a set must also fit the PEAK week's costs (the block's last week: its Olympic sets, the strength rest of the VOL3 work lifts) - `timeOk` with a peak guard; the day-shed and contested-day retries of E-81 use the same guard | R3-SES-02 ('never squeeze ... into a day that can't hold them'); INFERRED |
| E-83 | Week 1 started at or above the level ceiling because every template exercise begins at its minimum sets (02: quads 18 = level cap 18, back 16 > 13; 'each group can only gain 2 sets in 12 weeks') | before the time check, when the template's minimum structure puts a line above its level start, a group leaves one full-body day (`volume.structureCut`, the D-68 group-day reduction applied to the start): the day's exercises of that group become room cuts - first a group off the day's own pattern (a knee day gives up hips / chest / shoulders, a hip day quads / back); never a group with a work lift (or its pair partner) that day, never the week's last rear-delt station, never calves, and only when the group keeps 2-4 exercises on another main day and its line can still reach its start and floor there. Split days keep their template (both exposures of a day type stay even). 02 now starts quads 14, back 10, chest 10 and climbs 14 -> 18, 10 -> 13, 10 -> 13 | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-04 (each group reaches its ceiling in the last week), R2-PER-03 / R2-PER-04; RECHECK 02 major |
| E-84 | The soccer player's hips ran 1.6-1.8 x every other group (glutes and hamstrings filled as two lines; 03) | outside the physique / strength plans (`rules.volume.one_target_groups`: modifiers field_off, strength_athlete_off, stay_active, general, fat_loss, athlete_in) a FOCUS group of two lines carries ONE weekly target: hips = the hamstrings line's; glutes follow as credit (`targets.fill.glutes = 'credit'`) unless the client picks glutes as a Focus muscle. Physique and strength plans keep two lines. 03 hips: 13.25 -> 17 (quads 11 -> 14) | COACH-R3 R3-SES-01 ('hips = glutes + hamstrings'); R2-VOL-06 |
| E-85 | Groups flat for 6-9 weeks while a full bank built up; week 2 added nothing (01 / 02 / 07) | whole sets follow the planned T(w) rounded half up (`rules.stairway.release_rounding.mode = 'half_up'`); while the week's lines together sit below the rounded sum of their targets, the line with the largest bank (>= 0.2) takes one set early (never past T + 0.8, never past its cap); the 'never stands still' smoothing keeps its >= 0.4 bank | COACH-ANSWER VOL-06 ('you round up, you round down'), COACH-R3 R3-VOL-04 (3 % then 5 %) |
| E-86 | 1-set bonus stations, new exercises mid-block (01 W4 / W10, 04, 06), and the optional Saturday unused while quads were 'not trained (time)' (01) | an exercise the stairway ADDS (bonus / Focus) opens at 2 sets (`rules.placement.added_slots.entry_sets`), never in the week-1 raise, and only where an exercise block starts (PER-02) - or, when the week's ceiling held it back there, on an optional day later in the same exercise block while it still runs 2+ weeks and the line stays within a set of next week's target (one new exercise per line per exercise block). A big line may climb on a 2-set Focus exercise on the optional day when the main days are full (`big_lines_on_optional_day`). The 2-set entry runs at most a set ahead of the exercise block's last planned week; when it would pass the line's weekly limit the line's last exercise on the SAME main day gives one set (never a set the main-day floor needs; the donor keeps its peak allocation and takes the set back later). The week's 7 % ceiling counts what it measures - the main days (`totalRes(mainOnly)`); an optional-day exercise stays inside the all-days ceiling V15 checks (below 10 %). The time check's verification fails a block whose main-day total holds while its climb sits only on the optional day (H21 total_flat; sweep water polo 4 x 30) | COACH-ANSWER VOL-07, VOL-16, ORD-11 (2-3 sets), PER-02, UNI-05; COACH-R3 R3-VOL-03 (a bonus exercise on the optional day); R2-VOL-01 |

An added compound sits before its block's isolations and a horizontal pull / push before the block's vertical one (`generate.withAddedSlots`) | COACH-ANSWER VOL-07 ('adding sets to the things that matter most'), ORD-01 / ORD-02; RECHECK R02-9 |

Every eligible isolation / joint-care pair of a day is recommended (`week.pairRemaining`: within a block, never one group, two lower isolations only quad + leg curl, never two joint-care items, the day's two core exercises always; `generate.pairLeftovers`: an exercise whose layout partner was cut pairs with its neighbour - in its block, or the joint care at the head of the next block). ### E (fix round 1 / F2): athletic work (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/fix-r1-f2.mjs`)

Fix round 1 / F2 answered the coach-eye reviews of the 8 sample plans in the athletic area (jumps, throws, sprints, COD, Olympic
lifts, the plyo share, the Receive-Create stages; `engine/athletic.mjs`, the athletic parts of `select.mjs` and `dose.mjs`, minimal
seams in `week.mjs`, `generate.mjs`, `shape.mjs` and `validate.mjs`). Model data in the amendment layer `tools/fixture/fix-r1-f2.mjs`
(`amendRulesF2`, `amendSportF2`, `UI_F2`), applied by both builds after `fix-r1-f1.mjs` (`build-fixture.mjs`, `compile-model.mjs`,
`tools/compile/i18n.mjs` - one import and one call each). Regression tests: `tests/review-f2.test.mjs` (`R<sample>-<slug>` per review
issue, `F2-sweep-<intake>-<slug>` for a corpus intake the round's own changes broke on the way). Review log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md`
v0.4 fix round 1 / F2. Every value is PROPOSED unless the row says otherwise.

| # | Question | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-97 | The plyo share was a label, not a rule (03: 85 % plyometric against 45 %; 01: 29 % against 10 % in the Land stage) | the week's planned share (reactive jumps + ankle work over all jump and throw work) is held within `rules.athletic.f2.share.tolerance` (5 points) of its target by the athletic stairway (`planAthletic > holdShare`): over it, the ankle work and then the reactive jumps come down to their minimum dose, then the ankle block runs one set (`slot_plan[].sets`, applied by `generate.finishWeek`), then the ballistic jumps and throws go up - never past the week's own target of the key; under it, the ankle work and reactive jumps go up inside their bounds (the ballistic jumps and throws are never cut to raise the share). The never-drop raise keeps the share; a week the share or a coarse step kept below the week before brings the earlier weeks down to it (a backward pass, the ankle block one set fewer when needed). A goal the share lowered is what the week plans (V27 `under` reads it). A client who cannot throw (no medball / slam ball) holds the share over the jump work only (`athleticBudget`). An ankle set may be as short as 6 contacts (`reconcile.per_set_min.low_contacts` 6, was 10). V27 adds `share_over`: a week over the tolerance while a planned plyometric item still sits above its minimum dose (or the ankle block runs 2+ sets) fails. Samples: 01 9-21 % (10 -> 25), 02 30-55 % (35 -> 50), 03 50-65 % (45 -> 60), 08 39-50 % (35 -> 50); corpus (378 intakes): 39 of 1,810 athletic weeks over the tolerance, all at the minimum doses (low-share stay-active profiles: golf, lap swim, surf); 414 under it - weeks with no plyometric station left (a short stay-active session whose ankle block the time check cut, E-98 / E-108) or a Receive-stage week whose ballistic jumps the share never cuts | COACH-R2 R2-ATH-01 / R2-ATH-02, COACH-R3 R3-ATH-01 / R3-ATH-04; tolerance and minimum PROPOSED |

Higher-tier tracks place first (04 tiers); an added station goes to the main day with the most room in the 12 + 2 count, then (jumps / throws) the fewest jump / throw stations inside the R3-SES-01 day split (cross 2, same 4), then the day farthest from the track's other stations; only the profile's top-tier track (T3+) may take a full day's place. The Olympic budget trim now runs before the tracks (it reads the template's own stations; E-96's 'most athletic stations' ignores the added ones). ### E (fix round 2 / F1): time, volume and structure (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/fix-r2-f1.mjs`)

Fix round 2 / F1 answered the second coach-eye reviews of the 8 sample plans (grades D ... B-) in the time, volume and structure area
(`engine/intake.mjs`, `shape.mjs`, `volume.mjs`, `week.mjs`, `generate.mjs`, `validate.mjs`, `timefit.mjs`). Minimal seams outside the
area, each explained in its row: `render.mjs` (the test dates, a circuit's switchable station by name), `dose.mjs` (the circuit
'work time grows' range), `tools/fixture/build-fixture.mjs`, `tools/compile-model.mjs`, `tools/compile/i18n.mjs` (one import + call
each for the amendment layer). Every number below is PROPOSED unless its evidence column says otherwise; all of it is model data in
`tools/fixture/fix-r2-f1.mjs`. Regression tests: `tests/review-r2f1.test.mjs` (R<sample>-<slug>). D-81 (the `athletic.3` S1 / S2
same-pattern note) is **retired** by E-137.

| # | Issue (review) | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-135 | The whole climb of the big lines sat on the optional Saturday while the main days held flat for 10 weeks (01 / 03 blockers, 06 major) | `placement.added_slots.big_lines_on_optional_day = 'focus_only'`: only a Focus line may open an exercise on an optional day, inside that day's two Focus slots x 2-3 sets (`optional_focus {slots: 2, sets_max: 3}`); every other big line climbs on its main-day exposures (alternating the days); a small line keeps its short bonus exercise (R3-VOL-03). The probe, the floor checks and the ceilings read the days the stairway is placed on (`stair_sets_by_group`). An engine-added slot is picked with its day (generate `fillFor` passes the day's layout) | COACH-R3 R3-VOL-03 ('Monday's first back exercise, then Thursday's'; small-muscle extras on the optional day); COACH-ANSWER ORD-10 / ORD-11 |

### E (fix round 2 / F2): athletic work (engine 0.4.0, model 1.3.0; `tools/fixture/fix-r2-f2.mjs`)

Fix round 2 / F2 answered the second coach-eye reviews of the 8 sample plans (grades D ... B-) in the athletic area (jumps, ankle
work, throws, sprints, COD, Olympic lifts, the plyo share, the WODs and the circuit's ball station, the swimmer's shoulder care):
`engine/athletic.mjs`, the athletic parts of `select.mjs`, `dose.mjs` and `budget.mjs`. Minimal seams outside the area, each named
in its row: `generate.mjs` (the items and rooms handed to `planAthletic`, applying its set changes and minutes, the plan fields),
`week.mjs` (a timed test takes its station's place and day; a warm-up ankle day and a replaced station cost no minutes; the prehab
time class), `validate.mjs` (V27 / V19 read the new behaviour), `tools/fixture/build-fixture.mjs`, `tools/compile-model.mjs`,
`tools/compile/i18n.mjs` (one import + calls each for the amendment layer). Model data: `tools/fixture/fix-r2-f2.mjs`
(`amendRulesR2F2`, `amendSportR2F2`, `amendExerciseR2F2`, `UI_R2F2`, `R2F2_FINAL`), applied by both builds after `fix-r2-f1.mjs`.
Regression tests: `tests/review-r2f2.test.mjs` (`R<sample>-<slug>` per review issue, `R2F2-sweep-<intake>-<slug>` for a corpus
intake the round's own changes broke on the way). Review log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4 fix round 2 / F2. Every value is
PROPOSED unless the row says otherwise.

area: `engine/select.mjs` (non-athletic), `engine/dose.mjs` (non-athletic), `engine/render.mjs`, `engine/explain.mjs` and the words
(i18n through the amendment layer). Minimal seams outside the area, each named in its row: `engine/generate.mjs` (the second selection
pass: `run(deps, intake, model, ropts)`, fresh layouts per pass, `selectAll(base)`, the live stations, the frozen fallback; the added
slots' `prev_added` / `base_added` / `prev_week_added` / `week_used` context), `engine/validate.mjs` (two lines: the MYO check reads
the same `never_tags` / `never_id_re` lists as dose), `tools/fixture/build-fixture.mjs`, `tools/compile-model.mjs`,
`tools/compile/i18n.mjs` (one import + calls each for the amendment layer). Model data: `tools/fixture/fix-r2-f3.mjs`
(`amendRulesR2F3`, `amendExerciseR2F3`, `UI_R2F3`, `R2F3_FINAL`, `TEXT_R2F3`, `ES_PRIORITY_R2F3`), applied by both builds after
`fix-r2-f2.mjs`. Regression tests: `tests/review-r2f3.test.mjs` (`R<sample>-<slug>` per review issue, `R2F3-sweep-<intake>-<slug>`
for a corpus intake the round's own changes broke on the way). Review log: `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` v0.4 fix round 2 / F3. Every
value is PROPOSED unless the row says otherwise.

| # | Issue (review) | Decision | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-178 | Selection runs before the time check: its block changes and laterality flips landed on stations the time check then dropped, the dial counted stations that never trained (01 / 03 V28), and every new selection rule moved the time check's knife edge (a 2-3 min shift changed the trained groups of 01 and 04) | **a second selection pass**. Pass 1 picks exactly as before (the round's rules are off: `E.S2` is null without `ctx.live`); the volume engine says which stations are live (sets in some week); pass 2 picks the live resistance / isolation / Focus / core stations again with the round's rules (`rules.selection.r2`), each pick held to the **profile of its pass-1 pick** (`like`: the same two-arm / one-arm class and the same credit class - a timed circuit's upper station only its credit class), so the minutes and the credit lines the time check proved still hold; athletic, joint-care, warm-up and cardio stations, dropped stations and fallback picks (`pattern_gap`, `no_candidate`, `machine_iso_swap`, `no_medball_sub`) copy the pass-1 fill (a home-substitute or out-of-band push-up stand-in is picked again inside the profile). Only the dial's flips and the knee-flexion conversion (E-183) change a profile: when the second volume pass then loses a trained group or adds a group below its floor, pass 2 is redone **frozen** (pass-1 lateralities, no conversion); when the finished plan with flips fails an error check, `run` builds the frozen plan and returns the one with fewer failing error checks. Every pass runs on fresh layouts (the volume engine marks slots). `select.SECOND_PASS`; `selection.r2.second_pass`, `base_pick_bonus` 2, `repeat_in_week` -1.5. `generate.mjs` seam. 01 takes the frozen path (its V28 warning stays) | COACH-R3 R3-SES-02 (the time check decides what trains); PER-02; PROPOSED (the two-pass design, weights) |
| E-179 | The upper-body two-arm share sat at 58-65 % against 20-30 % for a linebacker and a midfielder, and at 53-58 % against 50 / 80 / 90 % for a physique client (02 major, 03 minor, 06 major) | **the laterality dial reads the upper stations too** (second pass only): `decideUpperLaterality` turns the fewest stations from their pass-1 laterality toward the block's share (`D10.upper.by_exercise_block`), one way only - too two-armed: the week's later exposures turn one-arm first (rows, pulldowns, overhead presses; a bucket only as many as the kit holds different one-arm lifts); too one-armed: the first exposures turn two-arm first. ## G. v0.5 (engine 0.5.0, work package V1 "structure and volume"; 2026-10-11)

Source of every decision: `design/ENGINE_V05_PLAN.md` (binding), COACH-CT CT-01, COACH-R4 (R4-SES-01 … 05, R4-PRG-01 … 04), CX-01 … CX-04,
`reviews/claude-codex-audit.md`. Items marked PROPOSED are the engine's own numbers where the coach gave none; they are listed again in the
package report as open questions.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-01 | **No time fitting** (CT-01, R4-SES-01). `engine/timefit.mjs` is deleted with the time check, the cutters, the minutes-based group dropping, the 100 % / 110 % rules and the time offers. Deleted rules: `session.exercise_cap_by_minutes / minutes_default / minutes_allowed / long_day / time_fit / athlete_cap_hard`, `cut_order`, `time_model.skill_in_warmup_short`, `volume.pattern_keep / per_session`, `stairway.lambda / capacity / track / release / hold_rules / min_climb / limits.*` (except `pain_after_freeze`), `conditioning.wod.short_minutes`, `supersets.triggers.short_session`. `schedule.session_minutes` in an intake is accepted and ignored with `warning.session_minutes_retired`. The minutes of a day are an **estimate only** (`engine/estimate.mjs`, `rules.time_model.use = 'estimate_only'`). The time-fitting words are deleted from both languages (`REMOVED_V05`). | `tools/fixture/v05-structure.mjs`, `engine/intake.mjs`, `engine/week.mjs`, `engine/volume.mjs`, `engine/generate.mjs` |
| G-02 | **V3 is now "no time fitting"** (error): a plan that carries `time_check`, a time offer, `session_minutes`, a day `minutes_limit` or a retired time warning fails. | `engine/validate.mjs` v3 |
| G-03 | **Session size by exercise count** (R4-SES-02): `rules.session.size.by_type` - general 5-7 (7), aesthetics 6-8 (7), strength 5-6 (6), bootcamp 5-7 stations (7), athletic 6-12 (12), older 5-6 (6). Every counted station has a `rank`; a day holds the stations with `rank <= count`; `count` = the day type's own `count`, else the type's default, inside the range. Outside the count: warm-up, the 2 core exercises, joint care, skill practice, conditioning, cardio, cool-down, test rows and the quick ankle prep block. | `rules.session.size`, templates `rank` / `count` / `skeleton`, `week.keepStation` |
| G-04 | **The coach's skeletons** (R4-SES-05; OBSERVED BYOP): A = row, squat, pulldown, lunge, biceps, quad isolation, calves (7), rear delt (8th); B = press, hinge, vertical / incline press, single-leg hinge, triceps, leg curl, side delt (7), calves (8th); the 3-day builder's C day; athletic days = the athletic block, then the A / B / C lifts with no arm isolation; push / pull / legs. Applied to aesthetics 2-6, `ppl_x2`, fat-loss / no-equipment 2 and 4 day, athletic 2-6 and their variants. Left as written (ranked only): strength, hybrid / class, bootcamp circuits, `baseball_parts`, `hyrox`. | `amendTemplatesV05` |
| G-05 | **The 2-day rule** (R4-SES-03): on 2 training days every big group starts at 6 and tops at 8 weekly sets (+ the Focus add), the small groups take the same numbers at most; the plan says so (`warning.two_day_rule`) and suggests a third day; a 2-day maintenance plan holds no line above 8. The floor of 10 applies from 3 training days, counted on all days. | `volume.targetsFor`, `rules.volume.two_day`, V22 |
| G-06 | **One target per group** for every client type: glutes + hamstrings carry one hips target on the carrier line (hamstrings, or glutes when glutes is the Focus). | `rules.volume.one_target_groups` |
| G-07 | PROPOSED sizes where the coach gave no number: stay-active (non-competitive athletic) 5-8 (8); CrossFit-style class 5-7 (7). | `rules.session.size.by_type` |

| G-09 | PROPOSED: bootcamp rounds by level 3 / 4 / 5 (PRG-03 "3 to 5 rounds"; was by minutes). The circuit work seconds keep the coach-reviewed approved steps (35 … 46 s over 8 weeks). The ball station still shares the week's throw target. | `rules.supersets.bootcamp.stairway.rounds_by_level`, `shape.circuitWorkSeconds` |
| G-10 | **Progression kernel** moved from `design/progression/` to `engine/progression/` with the audit fixes (HIGH-2: rep and load steps are conditional; MEDIUM-1: edits rebuilt from allowlists; MEDIUM-5: superset saving bounded; loose tolerance refused; whole-set acceleration report). The 3 `todo` audit tests pass. | `engine/progression/*.mjs`, `tests/audit`, `tests/progression` |
| G-11 | **The stairway is whole sets per group** fitted to reach the final target (CX-01): `planStairway` (the 3 % / 5 % weights scaled by one lambda, which may exceed 1), the top number arrives in the last week (R4-PRG-01; the validator allows one week early), a week that adds more than about 15 % - a group by 2 or more whole sets (PROPOSED), or the week's total - carries a note (R4-PRG-02). The next block starts about 5 % above the last start, at least 1 set (PROPOSED), never above the top (R4-PRG-04). | `engine/progression/stairway.mjs`, `rules.stairway.progression`, `rules.cycles.next_block` |
| G-12 | **Allocation** (plan section "How volume is allocated"): a slot's sets belong to the group its exercise trains as a main mover; 2-5 sets an exercise (2-4 at L1); one more set goes to the exercise with the fewest sets, the earlier one on a tie (VOL-07), the day after the one used last (R3-VOL-03), main days before the optional day; calves 3 sets on every leg day that holds a calf exercise, 2-3 on an optional day; a group the slots cannot take to its target gets the coach's **bonus exercise** on an optional day (2-3 sets; at most 2 added a day and one a group - PROPOSED), opened at the exercise-block start that first needs it; with no optional day the group is capped and the plan says so. Helper credit 0.25, group credit at most 1.0 a set. | `volume.planWeeklySets` |
| G-13 | **Nothing dropped, everything said** (R4-SES-04). Every week of the schedule is holdable (a week's number is capped at what the slots hold that week); a group never asks for fewer sets than it held last week; after the block the printed schedule is reconciled with what the weeks held and fitted again so it still arrives in the last week. What the template cannot hold is said: `warning.capped_by_template`, `warning.below_floor_template` (with the floor it means: 10, or 6 on 2 days), `warning.credit_only_groups` (groups with no exercise of their own, including the lines a client type leaves to the compound lifts). A flat block (the year plan's cut quarter, maintenance) has top = start; a flat target the template cannot hold is lowered to what it holds and said. | `volume.planStairs`, `block_targets` |
| G-14 | The calves' "planned top" the plan reports is what the calves rule gives (VOL-17), not the level's stairway number. | `volume.planStairs` |
| G-15 | **Supersets are a recommendation** the person switches on (`prefs.supersets_for_fatigue`); no session length turns them on. Pairs are suggested only between neighbours (no reordering). | `week.supersetsActive`, `week.pairRemaining` |
| G-16 | **Plan edits** (CX-02 / CX-04): `applyPlanEdits(plan, edits, model)` - `set_sets`, `remove_exercise`, `add_exercise`, `set_block`; totals recomputed, `plan.edited = true`, `plan.edits`, the validation recomputed and stamped `edited`; where the edit leaves the template's rules is listed as conflicts (classes bound / plan_shape / info), never repaired. The generated plan is never mutated. | `engine/edit.mjs` |
| G-17 | `plan/2` (v0.5): no `offers`, no `time_check`, no session minutes; `edited`; `meta.session_size / two_day / time`; `meta.stairway[]` with `groups, credit_only_groups, group_start, group_top, planned_top, schedule, arrival_week, realized_arrival_week, realized_top, capped, below_floor, bonus, acceleration`; week `target_by_group, acceleration, credit_only_groups`; day `exercise_count, easy_to_remove` (no `minutes_limit`); item `progression`. | `engine/generate.mjs`, CONTRACTS |
| G-18 | **Checks that changed**: V13 = session size (and the absolute set cap); V15 / H10 = final sets reached in the last week, not more than a week early, never under the schedule; V18 = a calf exercise runs 3 sets on its leg day, core 2, rear delts with the rows; V22 / H17 = floor of 10 from 3 days, 6 for big groups on 2 days, exempt only when said; V23 / H18 = 1-4 exercises a group on a full-body day; H21 = targets and sets never down, last week hardest; H22 = every week's target is the planned schedule. An edited plan is not judged by the generated-plan size and schedule rules. | `engine/validate.mjs`, `UI_V05` labels |
| G-19 | **Athletic layer, forced by the new structure**: a sport track's station keeps its day inside the session size (the first station and the top-tier track may use the room up to the range maximum); a track opens a further station only when the block's lowest weekly target holds every station at its smallest dose (one throw station climbs 2 x 10 -> 2 x 20 instead of two stations at 40 from week 1); the ankle days open in week order (the first one starts in place, CAT-10); the ankle prep station is marked on its items (`ankle_block`) and stays outside the count whatever drill fills it; the athletic set climb no longer asks the day for minutes. | `engine/athletic.mjs`, `week.ensureThrowStation` |
| G-20 | Joint care that finds every main day at its maximum does its first weekly session on the optional day (it used to get there through the time cut). | `week.addPrehab` |
| G-21 | **Selection, forced by the new structure**: the class's skill piece is picked before the day's core (it sat in the warm-up); a nearest-pattern stand-in that is a second version of another exercise of its day takes its first alternative that is not. | `engine/select.mjs` |
| G-22 | `tests/fixtures/plan-min.json` (hand-specified, 3 weeks x 2 days) is re-specified for v0.5: no time fields, the 2-day rule (big groups 8, chest and shoulders 6 -> 8), one calf exercise x 3 sets a leg day, the v0.5 stairway record; the profile is marked competitive so the fixture carries the athletic session size. | `tests/fixtures/plan-min.json` |
| G-23 | **Open (8 `todo` tests)** - real effects of the new structure outside this package: athletic dose steps and pre-season sizing (4, `tests/review-r2f2`), selection week-families / hinge pair / pulldown pair / upper-body dial (4, `tests/review-r2f3`). Listed in the table below. | package report |
| G-24 | The model version stays 1.3.0 although rules, templates and words changed; the lead decides the bump when V2 - V4 land. | `model/CHANGELOG.md` |

### Tests converted, retired or left open in engine 0.5.0

Every test that asserted time-fitting behaviour was **retired** (its code replaced by a `// RETIRED in engine 0.5.0` comment in the test file) or
**converted** to the v0.5 rule; tests that still describe a rule the coach wants and that the new structure breaks outside this package are
**TODO (open)** (they run, are reported, and do not count as failures). New tests: `tests/v05-structure.test.mjs` (72, named by coach answer id).

Totals after V1: TODO (open) 8, converted 96, retired 69, todo -> passes 3. After V2 (table at the end of this section): TODO (open) 4 (the athletic layer), 4 TODO closed, 15 more converted, 0 retired.

### G (work package V2): library and selection (engine 0.5.0; `tools/fixture/v05-library.mjs`, `engine/library.mjs`; 2026-10-11)

Source: `design/ENGINE_V05_PLAN.md` "V2 library and selection", COACH-R4 R4-LIB-01 … 05, R4-SCP-03, `HANDOFF.md` step 5,
`reviews/codex-model-review.md` Appendix F1, `samples/RECHECK_v0.3.md` / `samples/REVIEW_LOG.md` (selection findings not about time).

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-25 | **Optional add-ons** (R4-LIB-01). The records the library marked `proposed_v1` / `proposed_v1_1` are live as `optional_addon`: 29 records + the 2 mid-range pull records of G-29. A template never picks one; a plan lists them as swaps (`item.swaps`) and offers the ones that fit the client (`plan.meta.addons`); the person adds one with a plan edit. **Neck work stays proposed** (2 records) and is never shown: not a pick, a swap or an offered add-on. The "31 proposed" are read by record status: the floor Y / T raise is `added_v1_1` (the coach added it in round 2, R2-LIB-07) and stays a library exercise although the question's wording named "prone Y/T". | `amendExerciseV2`, `rules.selection.addons`, `library.NEVER_PICK_STATUS` / `NOT_SHOWN_STATUS`, `select.hardFilter` step 3 |
| G-26 | **The two add-ons a template may pick**: wall balls and sled pushes, in a HYROX station circuit (R4-LIB-01 approved) and in the CrossFit-style class WOD (`families: hybrid_crossfit`; PROPOSED reading of "maybe in the CrossFit program": **on**, one flag `enabled` turns it off). In the WOD a wall ball stays off the squat day like the thruster. A HYROX circuit picks those two and the coach's own SkiErg / row / burpee stations; the other race stations of R3-ATH-03 (sled pull and drag, burpee broad jumps, farmer carry, sandbag lunges) are its swaps. **R4-LIB-01 supersedes R3-ATH-03 and R3-ATH-01 on which record is the pick** (round 4 > round 3). | `rules.selection.addons.primary_exceptions`, `library.addonPrimaryOk`, `select.rankHyrox` / WOD order |
| G-27 | **Joint care** takes the code's own live exercise; a code whose exercises are all add-ons holds no station (the racket elbow, balance), and no stand-in from another code's list fills it. Swimmer shoulder care every session (R3-ATH-01) = KB face pull / floor Y-T raise, external rotation as swap and offered add-on. **Achilles / calf** (R3-ATH-02 "calf and Achilles work every leg day"): its two named exercises are add-ons, so the station takes one of the coach's calf raises (standing DB, machine, body-weight deficit) on a leg day with no calf exercise; a day that already holds a calf exercise gets no second calf station. `rules.prehab.preferred_status_ok` is deleted. | `select.pickFor` (prehab), `week.addPrehab`, `generate.collectWeek`, `rules.prehab.preferred_by_code.ACHILLES_CALF` |
| G-28 | **`plan.meta.addons`** (PROPOSED shape and counts): up to 6, in order joint care of the profile's injury codes, the add-ons the library marks for the client's sport (a reactive COD profile first gets the reaction drill), then a farmer carry for general / athletic goals (LIB-19). Each passes the client's kit, age, pain and exclusion filters, is not already in the plan, and carries `{exercise_id, name_key, kind, code, bucket, block, sets [min,max], reps, counts_as_muscle_sets, video, why}`. Cardio add-ons dosed in free text (distance pieces, ruck walk) are not offered (no bilingual dose); they stay addable. | `select.offeredAddons`, `generate.buildAddons`, `render.addonsSection` |
| G-29 | **Carries and sled work** (R4-LIB-02): 2-4 trips of 20-40 m or 20-40 s, never reps (`reps.type = 'distance_or_time'`, per side when one-sided; a stationary hold is seconds); credit class `NONE`; the `FUNCTIONAL` bucket's block is `conditioning`; an added carry lands in the conditioning block. The coach's own suitcase carry (reference record, a core pick for a low-back hot spot) follows the same rule: it stays in the core block, by distance or time, with no credit. | `amendExerciseV2`, `rules.conditioning.carry`, `dose.doseItem`, `edit.applyPlanEdits`, `render.formatReps` |
| G-30 | **Mid-range pull** (R4-LIB-03): pattern `MRPULL`, tag `mid_range_pull`, between the horizontal and the vertical pull. Records: `high-row-machine`, `45-degree-cable-row` (PROPOSED records, optional add-ons, credit as a row) and the library's face pulls (tag only; they stay rear-delt isolations). The coach's templates have no mid-range slot, so a row or a pulldown lists one mid-range pull as its last swap when the kit holds one. **Incline first** (R4-LIB-03, R4-SES-05 slot 3): for goals muscle, general, fat loss and the non-competitive "stay athletic" adult (PROPOSED: counted as general population) an incline press of the vertical / mid-range slot scores +4 (landmine / viking +2), the slot stays two-arm (the upper dial turns rows and pulldowns instead), and overhead presses stay in the swaps. The incline presses of that slot are his VERTICAL PUSH column's ("Incline Seated DB / Barbell Press"), so the slot keeps its shoulders credit. The week never holds the library's two DB high-incline records. | `rules.selection.mid_range` / `incline_first`, `select.scoreOf`, `select.decideUpperLaterality` |
| G-31 | **Swap notes** (R4-LIB-04): `item.swap_notes[{id, keeps, loses}]` for every swap, from the catalog's own fields (credit class, implement, grip tags, laterality, range, support, plane, level); `item.instead_of {exercise_id, keeps, loses}` + `note.instead_of` when the pick stands in for an exercise the kit does not hold (the slot's best pick with every piece of equipment). The wording table `swap.keeps.*` / `swap.loses.*` is PROPOSED. | `library.swapNote`, `select.intendedWithFullKit`, `generate.buildItem`, `render.swapText` |
| G-32 | **Video** (R4-LIB-05): every catalog record's `video` is `{url, kind: 'search'}` built from its final English name; `item.video = {kind, url, copy_name}`; the page prints the link and "Name to copy into YouTube: `…`" (the English gym name in a Spanish plan). A cardio session is searched by its session name and has no record name to copy. `assertModel` refuses any other link. The library source `synthesis/02_exercise_catalog.json` was emptied of links too (336 `video_urls` arrays, 188 `raw_with_links` cells; nothing else changed). Kind `coach` is reserved for the coach's own videos. | `attachVideoSearchV2`, `library.videoOf`, `model.assertModel`, `render.itemDetails` |
| G-33 | **Profile confidence** (R4-SCP-03): `profiles.sports[*].confidence` + `confidence_basis`, derived from the 04 profile's own provenance - `confirmed_by_coach` (evidence OBSERVED, or the plan approved by name: swimming R3-ATH-01, HYROX R3-ATH-03), `similar_sport` (a base profile, a position delta or a reused variant), `proposed` (a needs analysis with his general rules). Counts: 23 / 67 / 37. Never printed on a plan. | `profileConfidence`, `amendSportV2`, `rules.render.profile_confidence` |
| G-34 | **Clean-ups** (HANDOFF step 5): (1) the 60+ and non-priority 3 % step caps are deleted from the rules and retired in `generator_spec_v1.json` (`older_adults_60plus_step_cap_retired`, rule `cap-step-older` action `none`); (2) the spec's "tables tuned later from users' data" note is retired; (3) `rules.cut_order` was deleted by V1 - a test now holds it out; (4) the 60+ flag (`older_ok`) is true for the landing drills round 3 switched on (stick the landing, low depth drop, DB snap down); high-impact, weighted, single-leg-or-weighted and unstated-height drops stay opt-in; the engine reads the flag; (5) the master doc files the Z-press as advanced. **Appendix F1**: split-step reaction drill = bouts of seconds, needs space (cones optional), no longer in the 60+ no-hop list; the 2 min / 1 min intervals read as time; carries as G-29; the Nordic / Copenhagen / calf / cuff records and the HYROX stations are settled by G-25 … G-27. | `amendExerciseV2`, `amendRulesV2`, `generator_spec_v1.json`, `BLAZE_CAMP_BRAIN.md` |
| G-35 | **Selection, coach-eye findings not about time**: the lead chest press (`lead_press.floor_penalty` -8; the one-arm penalty also in a body-weight kit with a two-arm push-up; a floor press that is also alternating pays both); a sissy squat is its own movement; the second hinge of a hip day (`second_hinge`: a single-leg hip thrust for trained clients, else a glute bridge); the week cap of a movement family (-7); route drills and concrete block-change details were already rules - tests now hold them on 33 plans. | `rules.selection.v2`, `select.mjs` |
| G-36 | **Open (4 `todo` tests, all athletic layer, `tests/review-r2f2`)**: sprint-metre steps, the V27 early-peak mutation, the soccer upper-body plyo station, the pre-season cuts. Not library or selection. | package report |
