### Work package V3 "words and checks" (engine 0.5.0; 2026-10-11)

Source: `design/ENGINE_V05_PLAN.md` "V3 words and checks", COACH-R4 R4-SCP-01 / R4-SCP-02, R4-PRG-02 / R4-PRG-03, R4-SES-04, COACH-CT CT-01,
R4-SES-01, R2-GEN-02, R2-GEN-08. Model layer: `tools/fixture/v05-words.mjs` (after `v05-library.mjs`, in both builds). Items marked
PROPOSED are the engine's own words or numbers where the coach gave none.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-37 | **The liability gate** (R4-SCP-02 "they need to click it before the plan shows"; R4-SCP-01). `rules.legal.gate {required: true, field: release.accepted, before: plan_generated, applies_to: everyone}`; `generate` checks the release first (before the health screen), so a missing, false or non-boolean `release.accepted` gives `status: invalid`, no weeks, error `release_missing`; the plan's stamp says so (`release_stamp.accepted: true`, last key); V21 / H20 fail a plan whose stamp is missing, not accepted or of the wrong version, and a non-plan result that holds weeks; `assertModel` refuses a model whose gate is switched off. **Unchanged and open:** a health flag still gives `status: refer` (R2-GEN-03). R4-SCP-01 "everyone should be able to use the plan on their own" is read here as "everyone, once they accept the release"; whether a health flag should still stop the plan is a question for the coach (package report). | `engine/generate.mjs releaseStamp`, `engine/validate.mjs v21Release`, `engine/model.mjs assertModel` |
| G-38 | **The release line** (R4-SCP-01 / R4-SCP-02 "outputted in their program somewhere at the bottom or at the top: you've released liability"): `rules.legal.release_line`. A full plan and the overview print it on top (the line of the stamp's version: self-built, built by a fitness professional, a guardian for a minor) and at the bottom (`ui.plan.release_footer` + the standing disclaimer); the block / week / day views and every fragment print the short line; all with the link to `DISCLAIMER.md` / `DISCLAIMER.es.md`. A refer or invalid result keeps the plain use-at-your-own-risk line (no plan was built). PROPOSED wording, a draft for attorney review like the disclaimer. | `engine/render.mjs releaseKeys / ownRisk / ownRiskShort / releaseFooter` |
| G-39 | **Progression as a rule the person applies** (R4-PRG-03, CX-02 / CX-03): every lift with `item.progression` prints one line - "Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with N left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8." (N = the item's own planned reps in the tank); a fixed-rep lift drops "start again"; a lift with its reps written per set (the 12/10/8 pyramid) reads "the written reps" (PROPOSED wording for these two). The section "How you progress" says the rule with an example from the plan, what the sets do (CX-02: same sets = progress with reps or weight), the weeks that add more than usual, where the next block starts (R4-PRG-04) and that the plan is an editable template (CX-04). No number is promised for a week; `assertModel` refuses `promised_numbers` other than false. | `rules.render.progression`, `render.progressionLine / progressSection` |
| G-40 | **The acceleration note** (R4-PRG-02): a week whose entry the volume engine wrote is marked `↑` in the stairway table with one legend line, said on its week ("This week jumps more than usual (17 % more sets for Shoulders): adjust if needed.") and listed in "How you progress"; the notice up front stays. PROPOSED: the mark. | `rules.render.acceleration`, `render.accelLines` |
| G-41 | **The time estimate is information** (CT-01, R4-SES-01 "an estimate like about 60-75 minutes"): a day prints "Average time: about 80-95 min (an estimate, for information only)"; the range is the estimate 10 % each way, rounded to 5 min (PROPOSED width); "How to adjust" says nothing is cut or changed because of time. The plan field stays `est_minutes`. | `rules.render.estimate`, `render.estText` |
| G-42 | **Easy-to-remove hints** (R4-SES-04 "they get to pick"): a day prints "Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: … · extra jump / throw stations: … · small-muscle accessories: …" from `day.easy_to_remove`, in the rule's order, at most 6 names; nothing is removed. | `rules.render.easy_to_remove`, `render.easyToRemoveLine` |
| G-43 | **Weekly sets per group, week by week** (plan "How volume is allocated" step 4): one 3-column table per training block - group, the sets of every week counted from the plan's own exercises (a dot between exercise blocks), the planned final sets ("13 of 25" for a group the template caps, "no exercise of its own" for a credit-only group) - in the block view, the full plan and the overview. The first / middle / last table stays. **Macro views** (R2-GEN-02): `renderPlan(..., {view: 'week' or 'day', macro: true})`, `renderWeek / renderDay(..., {macro: true})` give the week (level, notes, sets per group, days at a glance, one compact table per day) and the day (one table) for a screenshot; the block view was already a macro view. Tables stay at 4 columns or fewer. | `render.setsByWeekTable / weekMacro / dayMacro` |
| G-44 | **The v0.5 check list**: V30 template skeleton + nothing dropped (R4-SES-05, R4-SES-04), V31 optional add-ons (R4-LIB-01), V32 incline first (R4-LIB-03, warn), V33 no third-party video links (R4-LIB-05), V34 the progression rule (R4-PRG-03), V35 the acceleration note (R4-PRG-02), V36 the easy-to-remove list (R4-SES-04), V37 carries by distance or time (R4-LIB-02); V21 is the liability gate. `rules.validation.v05.by_rule` maps every v0.5 rule to its checks (exercise count V13, final sets and arrival V15 / H10, the floor from 3 days and the 2-day rule V22 / H17, no time fitting V3). No check reads minutes. 59 check ids. | `engine/validate.mjs`, `rules.validation` |
| G-45 | **`day.skeleton`** (plan/2, after `exercise_count`): the coach's skeleton the day was built to, copied from the template day type (`gym_A` … `ppl_legs`, else null). V30 holds a day to the skeleton it names: the template's own skeleton, every template lift inside its slot's pattern (knee / hip / pull / push - the laterality dial or a kit stand-in may change the bucket inside the pattern), the compounds in the coach's order, and every counted lift present in a stairway week unless the day says why (an exclusion, pain, play spacing, the 60+ default), the slot's group is said to be credit only, or the kit holds no exercise for the slot. The benchmark scorer reads the template itself, so a day cannot escape by naming no skeleton. **Open:** the hand-specified `tests/fixtures/plan-min.json` keeps its pre-R4-SES-05 mixed days (squat + row + hinge + press on one day) and names no skeleton, so V30 does not judge it; re-specifying it means rewriting about 25 validator mutation cases. | `engine/week.mjs`, `engine/generate.mjs`, `validate.v30Skeleton` |
| G-46 | **V32 is a warn check** read as "at least half of the vertical / mid-range push picks of a training block are incline or mid-range presses, when the kit holds an incline press" (PROPOSED reading of "bias incline pressing … we should have that variability"); the class model's work press and circuit stations are not read; a failing plan tells the client in plain words (`check.failed.V32`). | `rules.validation.v05.incline` |
| G-47 | **V13 keeps the upper bound only.** A generated session under its range's minimum is legitimate in several cases (a WOD-only class day, a HYROX station day, an end-test week, a 60+ day whose jumps are off, a kit with no exercise for a slot), so the lower bound is a soft benchmark metric (`session_size_under`), not a validator error. | `validate.v13Caps`, `design/bench/score.mjs` |
| G-48 | **Benchmark scorer 2.0** (`design/bench/score.mjs`, `bench-score/2`): every minutes-based metric is deleted (the re-cost of a day, days over the minutes, the 100 % / 110 % bands, session-length and bootcamp-minute checks, day spread, slack) with the time-check policy metrics (offers, groups out for time, keep order, below-floor states, needed supersets) and the rate limits CX-01 retired (7 % steps, 10 % weekly rise, minimum climb, flat-week counts, early-step and week-1-ceiling rules, per-session set caps, exercise caps by minutes); 40 metrics removed, 19 added for the v0.5 rules (`time_fields`, `skeleton_mismatch`, `template_lift_missing`, `session_size_over / _under`, `exercises_per_group`, `target_not_reached`, `arrival_early`, `below_schedule`, `two_day_rule_misses`, `unsaid_cap`, `addon_picks`, `incline_share`, `liability_gate`, `video_links`, `progression_rule_missing`, `acceleration_unnoted`, `easy_to_remove_invalid`, `carry_dose`); floors are read on all days from 3 training days, a group the plan SAYS its template cannot hold is reported, not counted; acceptance rows A1-A14. `run.mjs` is rewritten without the schedule offers, the session-length sweep and the minutes trace (default label `v05`; it refuses to compare against a scorer 1.x result); `selftest.mjs` breaks one thing per v0.5 rule (49 checks). `results_baseline.json` / `BASELINE.md` are the engine 0.4 run under scorer 1.0, kept as history. | `design/bench/` |
| G-49 | **Dead words deleted** (17 keys, `REMOVED_V3`): the schedule section's lines (`ui.plan.trained_directly*`, `not_trained_*`, `calves_line*`), `time.kept`, `warning.offer_longer_sessions`, the unused estimate words of V1 and the three retired rate-cap reasons (3 % / 7 %). `ui.plan.weekly_sets_groups_note` no longer speaks of "a kept movement". | `tools/fixture/v05-words.mjs` |
| G-50 | **Spanish review** (R2-GEN-08; `ES_REVIEW_V3`, 41 lines): one word per idea - `liberación de responsabilidad` (never `descargo`), `en reserva` (never `de sobra`), `revisión de salud` for the health check and `seguimiento semanal` for a check-in; `repeticiones` inside sentences; neutral words with the gym's English word in brackets (`bandas elásticas (bands)`, `saltos de profundidad (depth jumps)`, `bisagra de cadera (hinge)`, `balón medicinal (wall ball)`). The 733 exercise names, cues, sport / profile names and day labels were not reviewed line by line (843 are still machine translation, `model/i18n/es_review.json`). Every Spanish line stays PROPOSED until the coach approves it. | `tools/fixture/v05-words.mjs`, `tools/compile/i18n.mjs` |

### Work package V4 "integrate" (engine 0.5.0; 2026-10-11)

Source: `design/ENGINE_V05_PLAN.md` "V4 integrate". Found by reading the 8 regenerated samples against the coach's sheets
(`dumps/BYOP`) and by the benchmark run of the 94 + 8 intakes (`design/bench`). Model layer: `tools/fixture/v05-integrate.mjs` (after
`v05-words.mjs`, in both builds). Items marked PROPOSED are the engine's own numbers or readings where the coach gave none.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-51 | **The skeleton keeps its laterality** (R4-SES-05 "horizontal pull, bilateral, symmetrical ... knee-dominant movement, bilateral ... knee-dominant movement, unilateral"; OBSERVED BYOP "Bilateral KD", "Unilateral KD", "Bilateral HD", "Unilateral HD"). On a gym A / B / C day and a push / pull / legs day the laterality dial (UNI-02 / UNI-03) no longer turns a slot: sample 06 used to hold a pendulum squat in the lunge slot and a back extension in the single-leg hinge slot from week 5, and a one-arm row in slot 1. Now a two-leg slot holds a two-leg lift and a single-leg slot a single-leg lift in every exercise block, the A day's first row is a two-arm row, and the home second squat stays two-legged (a goblet squat is "fine on the second day", R2-SES-02). R4 is newer than UNI-02, so it wins where they meet. INFERRED and open for the coach: the athletic days (`athletic_A / B / C`) keep the dial, because UNI-02 / UNI-03 ask for 80-90 % single-leg work for field and court athletes. V30 holds the rule (`slot_laterality`); V28 reads the share on the stations the dial may move. | `rules.selection.skeleton_laterality`, `select.decideLaterality / decideUpperLaterality / scoreOf`, `validate.v30Skeleton / v28LateralityShare` |
| G-52 | **A Focus slot never pushes a group over its final sets** (R4-PRG-04 "never above the level's top number"; ORD-11 "Focus ... used to bring a priority muscle up to its weekly target"). Benchmark: a 16-year-old guard's hips sat at 22 weekly sets from week 1 against a final 16, flat for 12 weeks, because four optional-day Focus slots were filled for the sport's priority group. Where a group's exercises at their fewest sets hold more than its planned final sets, the optional day's Focus slots of that group close (last one first) while the group still starts at or above its level's start; the group then climbs on its main-day lifts. The person's own Focus muscle keeps the slots that fit. The main days' template lifts never close (R4-SES-04); a bonus exercise is not a Focus slot. PROPOSED. Recorded in `meta.stairway[].focus_closed`. 8 of the 94 benchmark plans change. | `rules.placement.optional_focus.close_when_over_top`, `volume.planStairs` |
| G-53 | **Push / pull / legs prints in the order of his sheet** (OBSERVED BYOP PUSHPULLLEGS). A day prints block by block, so the dips (a compound) printed before the flies and the single-leg hinge before the leg extension and the leg curl. The flies, the leg extension and the leg curl now sit in the strength block of their day. The two triceps slots pick their kind ("Tricep - Push": a push-down or kickback; "Tricep - Overhead": an overhead extension or skullcrusher - PROPOSED reading) by a preference weight. | `amendTemplatesV4`, `rules.selection.slot_variants`, `select.scoreOf` |
| G-54 | **The speed-session order holds** (`rules.ordering.hard` SPEED_SESSION_ORDER: sprint, jump, ankle, throw, cuts). The jump + throw superset suggestion used to move the throw up next to the jump, ahead of the ankle station (29 of the 102 benchmark plans). The pair is now marked only between neighbours and never reorders the block: the throw pairs with the lower-body jump station that stands right before it (the jump, or the ankle hops). | `athletic.pairAthleticBlock` |
| G-55 | **Older adults and "no floor work"**. Benchmark: a 74-year-old beginner with body weight only and "no floor work" got handstand skill work three times in one circuit, glute bridges and slider curls. (1) `handstand-skill-work-h-s` was filed as a foundational, low-skill lift that is fine for older adults: it is now advanced, high skill, never for the 60+ default; the wall handstand push-up is never for the 60+ default either (PROPOSED correction of an INFERRED level). (2) The exclusion covered the floor core records and every push-up; it now also covers the lifts done lying, sitting or kneeling on the floor, and it no longer covers the push-up with the hands on a bench (INFERRED id lists until the catalog carries a position field). Open: the library holds almost no standing core exercise, so the core block of a body-weight "no floor work" plan still uses floor drills. | `amendExerciseV4`, `rules.selection.floor_work`, `select.hardFilter` |
| G-56 | **Every main day opens with a warm-up** (ORD-08 "always"). The 5-day hybrid week's Friday was written "easy aerobic first" and had no warm-up station; the plan opened with the core block. It now holds the general warm-up line like every other main day (the aerobic block stays where the engine places it). | `amendTemplatesV4` |
| G-57 | **Benchmark scorer 2.1** (`design/bench/score.mjs`). Four readings of scorer 2.0 disagreed with rules the engine already applies on purpose; each is now read the engine's way and documented: the MYO-last check uses the engine's exclusions (never on a barbell or axial-high lift, a Nordic curl, a sissy squat or a Copenhagen, never on calves for an Achilles hot spot; E-53, E-193) - 46 placements were wrongly counted; the end-test week of a block runs the baseline week's athletic dose on its tested days and the class runs no Olympic work before a strength test (E-165, E-172, R2-PER-07) - the fall is reported and not counted unless it goes more than the reconcile tolerance under the first week (parameter `athletic_end_test_week_dose_ok`); a low-amplitude ankle hop is allowed in every Receive-Create stage whatever station it fills (R2-ATH-03, CAT-10); the laterality error is read on the stations the dial may move. New: `skeleton_mismatch` counts a wrong laterality in a pinned slot (kind `slot_laterality`); the plan table shows the template id. Also: the block view lists a day's rows in session order (`render.blockView`). | `design/bench/score.mjs`, `design/bench/run.mjs`, `engine/render.mjs` |

#### Tests converted in work package V4 (one-line reasons)

| File | Test | What | Reason (one line) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `compile.test.mjs` | counts: 733 kept ... 30 templates | converted | 1,678 -> 1,679 stations: the general warm-up of the 5-day hybrid week's Friday (ORD-08, G-56) |
| `review-f1.test.mjs` | R07-supersets-recommended | converted | a push day in the order of his sheet (press, press, incline, flies, dips, triceps, triceps) has no two neighbours that do not compete; pairs are only suggested between neighbours (G-15, G-53) |
| `review-f3.test.mjs` | R03-laterality-dial | converted | 06 / 07 are built on the coach's A / B and push / pull / legs days, whose slots name their laterality (R4-SES-05, G-51): asserts every lower slot keeps it in every week (was: later blocks hold more two-legged work) |
| `review-r2f3.test.mjs` | R06-upper-dial-toward-two-arm | converted | the A day's first row is a two-arm row from week 1 (R4-SES-05, G-51), so the two-arm share starts high: asserts 75 % or more in every block and that a two-arm station never turns one-arm (was: block 2 more than 5 points above block 1) |

No test was retired in V4. New tests: `tests/v05-integrate.test.mjs` (22 tests on each of the two models + 4 on the benchmark scorer + 1 on the
amendment module, named by coach answer id). The 4 `todo` tests of the athletic layer (G-23) are still open.

### Fix round 1 (engine 0.5.0; 2026-10-11)

Source: the coach-eye reviews of the 8 regenerated samples (01 B-, 02 C+, 03 C+, 04 C+, 05 B-, 06 C+, 07 B, 08 C). Every blocker and
major was fixed in the engine or its data, never in a plan. Complaints about session length were not acted on (CT-01). Model layer:
`tools/fixture/v05-fix1.mjs` (after `v05-integrate.mjs`, in both builds). Items marked PROPOSED are the engine's own numbers or
readings where the coach gave none.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-58 | **The athletic A / B / C days keep each slot's laterality** (R4-SES-05 slot 2 "knee-dominant, bilateral" / "a deadlift, a hip extension, or ... a hip thrust"; PER-04 "one day of squatting and the other day of leg pressing"). Samples 02 and 08: the two-leg slot was gone on both days (two single-leg knee lifts on Monday, two single-leg hinges on Thursday). G-51 had left this open; R4 is newer than UNI-02, so it wins. The second A day's slot 2 prefers a leg press, hack, belt or pendulum squat, the non-deadlift B day a hip thrust or a hip extension. A trained athlete's tested squat is a front or a back squat (02 had a Zercher squat). The first row of an athletic A day is a two-arm row. The 3-day builder's third day gets a two-leg knee lift that does not load the spine again. | `rules.selection.skeleton_laterality`, `rules.selection.x1.work_prefer / second_day_prefer`, `amendTemplatesX1`, `select.x1Score` |
| G-59 | **The "Chest / Shoulders" slot** (R4-SES-05 slot 3 "vertical push ... or an incline press"; BYOP label "Chest/Shoulders"; R4-LIB-03). Sample 06: chest sat at 13 weekly sets for 12 weeks against a planned 25, because both slot-3 presses counted for shoulders only. Now the week's first B day takes a 30-45 degree incline press there (it counts 1.0 for chest), when the week has two B days or is a 2-day plan and the kit holds a flat press for every pressing day. A high-incline press is credit class `MRPUSH`: 1 front delts, 0.5 chest, 0.25 triceps. **PROPOSED numbers - open for the coach.** | `rules.credit.classes.MRPUSH`, `rules.selection.x1.chest_shoulders`, `select.chestShouldersSlots` |
| G-60 | **The test replaces the lift's top set** (R2-PER-07). In a test week the tested lift opens the day's lifts (after the power block), it takes no stairway step that week, and its line reads "these N sets are your back-off sets". A lower-body test needs a heavy two-leg lift. | `rules.tests.x1`, `week.relocateStrengthTests / testedLiftFirst / strengthTestSlots`, `volume.addSet`, `validate.v12Order / V30` |
| G-61 | **The floor of 10 is read on the training days** (R2-VOL-06; ORD-10 "optional days ... bonuses"). Sample 01: a group held its floor only with the optional day and the plan did not say so. The main-day lifts hold the floor where they can; a group that needs the optional day is said up front (`meta.stairway[].floor_on_optional`, `warning.floor_needs_optional_day`). Where the training days' floor and the optional day's Focus slots together pass a group's final sets, a Focus slot closes (benchmark 88: biceps at 14.5 against a final 13). | `rules.volume.floor.counted_on`, `volume.allocateOnce / planStairs` |
| G-62 | **The steps are shared between the weeks** (R3-VOL-04 "small steps, then bigger"; R4-PRG-01 "reach the top in the last week, one week early at most"). Samples 01, 06, 08: every group took its last set in the same final week ("29 x 5, 30 x 6, then 34"). New kernel functions `stairwayProgress` (a group's curve) and `staggerStairways` (the week's total follows the sum of the curves; a group is never a whole set behind its own curve; at most half of the climbing groups arrive a week early). A group its template caps starts a little lower so it still climbs (`lowered_start`). The bonus exercise a group needs is in the block from week 1. A block that holds its sets (the year plan's cut quarter) keeps the number its first week holds. | `engine/progression/stairway.mjs`, `rules.stairway.progression.stagger`, `rules.placement.x1`, `volume.planStairs` |
| G-63 | **Pulling never falls under pressing for a sport that asks for it** (profile `pull_per_push`; VOL-18 "we need to balance this out"). Samples 02 and 03: pressing ran ahead of pulling, and shoulders ended above the back. Week by week the pulling sets are at least the pressing sets; the profile's own ratio (up to 1.25) sizes the back's final sets, so the back climbs to it. An athlete's shoulders line takes the sport's upper-body factor and never ends above the back. PROPOSED: the 1.25 ceiling and the shoulders rule. The block says when the balance is on (`meta.stairway[].push_pull`). | `rules.volume.push_pull`, `rules.volume.x1.shoulders_athlete`, `volume.addSet / allocateOnce / planStairs`, `intake.targetsFor` |
| G-64 | **Isolation exercises hold 3 sets** (VOL-16; VOL-07 "more biceps comes as a bonus exercise"; R4-SES-05 "a 7th-8th exercise is an extra small exercise at the end"). Sample 06: skullcrushers at 5 sets from week 1 next to a hip thrust at 3. An isolation exercise of a training day holds 2-3 sets and a 4th in the hardest weeks; what is left goes to the optional-day bonus exercise (2-4 sets), then to one extra small-muscle exercise at the end of a training day that already trains the group (2-3 sets). A Focus muscle's own exercises take their group's steps first (R3-SES-01: the glute lifts in sample 06 never gained a set). Side delts hold their own direct sets on a physique plan (R2-VOL-04; 4 / 6 / 8 by level, PROPOSED). Calves climb on the leg days when no optional day carries them (VOL-17; sample 07 sat at 6 for 12 weeks). The up-front "capped" line is kept for a Focus group, a group under its level's start and a big group under 80 %; the week-by-week table names every capped group (CX-04). | `rules.placement.x1`, `rules.placement.bonus.sets`, `rules.volume.x1.direct_min`, `rules.volume.calves.climb_without_optional`, `volume.runBlock` |
| G-65 | **The class week** (OT17 / OT18; PRG-10; R4-SES-05). Sample 04: the class press was a high-incline press at 2 sets, quads trained once, no triceps exercise. A class lift never holds fewer sets than his table starts at; the press is the standing strict press in both selection passes; the press day and the deadlift day get a triceps exercise; the deadlift day a single-leg knee lift. A for-time WOD grows by rounds and its cap follows; a barbell station prints the bar's load; the week's WODs hold a pull-up, an inverted row or toes-to-bar; jumping jacks are no station above L1; a station is never a second version of an exercise of its day. | `amendTemplatesX1`, `rules.placement.x1.work_min_from_hint_families`, `rules.conditioning.wod.x1 / for_time_progress`, `select.pickFor`, `dose` |
| G-66 | **The bootcamp sessions keep his five slots** (OBSERVED BYOP BOOTCAMP - OPTION 2). Sample 05: the station count cut his own slots and two days ran two circuits. His slots take the first ranks; the engine's additions fill the count where the floor is short (biceps with the pulls, triceps and a second chest station with the presses, a third press and a row). A day of 7 stations or fewer is one circuit. The week holds two rows (rear delts every week) and as much pulling as pressing. | `amendTemplatesX1` |
| G-67 | **The athletic numbers of a 2- or 3-day week** (samples 03 and 08). A profile the coach has not confirmed is written for his 4-day athletic sheet: a week of fewer days asks for three quarters of it. **PROPOSED: the 4-day reference and the 0.75 floor.** A pre-season week keeps the athletic work of the off-season block before it (R2-PER-06 "more speed and power"); V27 allows it. A change-of-direction sport jumps on every day that has a jump slot. In a heavy play week (R3-ATH-05) a day holds one jump station - a jump day and an ankle-hop day in the week - and a throw replaces the dropped cuts. A 50+ court player or a painful knee or ankle keeps the low box in every stage (R3-ATH-02, EQ-16). | `rules.athletic.x1`, `intake.athleticOf`, `athletic.planAthletic`, `week.heavyPlayStations`, `validate.v27` |
| G-68 | **Joint care lives in the template's own lift** (R4-SES-02; VOL-17; R3-ATH-02). Sample 08: a calf raise as joint care next to a dropped calf slot; a home leg extension as knee care on the hip day; 11 exercises a day against a printed 8. The calf raise, the leg extension and the rear-delt slot carry the care (item flag `joint_care`); an extra station is placed only on a day without that slot. The shoulder care of a racket player takes a face pull or a Y-T raise. A reference-only or program-only exercise is never joint care. The balance warm-up is the 60+ default. A non-competitive athletic adult's day is sized like his sheet (5-10, PROPOSED). | `rules.prehab.x1`, `rules.warmup.x1`, `rules.session.size.by_type.stay_active`, `week.addPrehab`, `select.pickable` |
| G-69 | **The MYO set** (VOL-03 "pain: cut, do not push it"; VOL-15). Sample 08: the last week's MYO set landed on the leg extension of a painful knee. Never on a muscle the plan cut for pain; a calf raise only when the day holds nothing else, and never in a week that tests sprints or jumps; a two-sided exercise before a one-side one; no intensifier on the optional day when a training day holds one. The plan explains the MYO set once. A training day may hold none. | `rules.effort.intensifiers`, `dose.intensifierEligibleRc`, `generate`, `validate.myoLastEligible` |
| G-70 | **Reps stay on his menu** (VOL-11; PER-11). Samples 02, 03, 06: a Zercher squat at 12-15 against its own 6-12, "7-9" and "5-8". A compound lift whose range runs past its own takes the nearest range of his REPS menu (4-6, 6-8, 8-10, 10-12, 12-15, 15-20). The client's own heavy choice (the heavy single-leg switch, UNI-04; the heavy lifting style, VOL-12) is never lifted back. A single-block late off-season moves to the heavier phase after its halfway week (PER-11; switched on). The three flies are isolation exercises (the library row said "compound"). | `rules.reps.rep_hint.x1`, `rules.reps.athlete_vol_phases`, `dose.capToHintMenu`, `amendExerciseX1` |
| G-71 | **Selection** (EQ-08; PER-02; PER-04; GEN-04; BYOP PUSHPULLLEGS). The leg-extension and leg-curl slots hold the machine while the gym has one (sample 07 used sissy squats and Nordics). "Tricep - Overhead" is an overhead extension on both push days, in every block. One bent-over barbell row a week. A lifter who names no stance tests the conventional deadlift. The lead chest press is a bench or machine press where the kit holds one. A lift keeps its credit class into the next exercise block (the sweep: a press slot turned into dips took a set from the shoulders). Every lift lists a swap; a high-incline and an overhead press are one swap category. Small kits (dumbbells and a bench) keep the incline press as the second day's lead press. A library lift used again in the week, with a named detail, is the pick before a reference-only or program-only record (R4-LIB-01), and the Kong row is no pick for a client without shoulder or back pain (LIB-09; sample 05 opened its week with it). | `rules.selection.x1`, `rules.selection.slot_variants`, `select.x1Score / pickable / pickFor / swapsR2` |
| G-72 | **The second selection pass** reads the plan's real sets in the laterality dials (sample 04: the lower two-leg share landed 15 points under its target). It keeps its picks while no group is lost, none falls under its floor and none ends more than one set under the first pass (there are no minutes to hold, CT-01; the old rule dropped every dial flip when a pick changed half a set of helper credit). PROPOSED. | `rules.selection.x1.dial_reads_plan_sets / second_pass_top_tolerance`, `generate.run`, `select.dialSets` |
| G-73 | **In season, the session on the eve of a game is the lighter one** (R2-ATH-07; the engine's own day rule "hard day 48 h before a game"). When the client's days put a session there, it holds at least one set fewer than the week's other training day. PROPOSED. | `rules.placement.x1.eve_of_game`, `volume.eveOfGame` |
| G-74 | **Benchmark scorer 2.2** (`design/bench/score.mjs`; listed in its header). Readings that follow rules the engine now applies on purpose: the tested lift first; a capped group named by the table; the MYO rule of G-69; joint care in a template lift; the upper two-arm share against the closest share the plan could reach (pinned stations and the library's one-arm lifts); every exercise against its own top in the staircase and exposure readings; push : pull by the model's own classes; a group whose own exercises all sit at their fewest sets. One reading is stricter: the floor on the training days. | `design/bench/score.mjs`, `design/bench/selftest.mjs` |
| G-75 | **Words** (R2-GEN-08). 13 new lines in English and Spanish (`UI_X1`): the test notes, the floor notice, the joint-care note, the MYO definition, the WOD notes, one line for several athletic shortfalls. The pickleball sample is also written in Spanish. Every Spanish line stays PROPOSED until the coach approves it. | `tools/fixture/v05-fix1.mjs`, `tools/compile/i18n.mjs`, `tools/generate-samples.mjs` |

### Fix round 2 (engine 0.5.0; 2026-10-11)

Source: the second coach-eye review of the 8 samples (01 B-, 02 B, 03 B-, 04 B-, 05 B-, 06 B, 07 B+, 08 B). Every blocker and major
was worked on in the engine or its data, never in a plan; complaints about session length were not acted on (CT-01). Model layer:
`tools/fixture/v05-fix2.mjs` (after `v05-fix1.mjs`, in both builds); the engine reads it as `rules.selection.x2`,
`rules.placement.x2`, `rules.athletic.x2`, `rules.conditioning.x2`, `rules.conditioning.buyout_pool.x2`, `rules.session.x2`,
`rules.supersets.bootcamp.x2`, `rules.olympic.x2`, `rules.core_block.x2`, `rules.volume.pain.x2`. Items marked PROPOSED are the
engine's own numbers or readings where the coach gave none.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-76 | **Core slots take the live library** (R4-LIB-01, R4-LIB-02; sample 01 major: a suitcase carry opened the swimmer's Friday). The suitcase carry is an optional add-on like the farmer carry. A carry, an above-level record or a swap-only record is a fallback only in a core slot; the low-back opener keeps dead bugs and bird dogs from his reference tab. Toes-to-bar from L3, no weighted core before the day's heavy lift, hanging core once a week (PROPOSED weights). | `rules.selection.x2.core_primary`; `select.mjs` pickable, `x2Score` |
| G-77 | **A low-back client repeats a plank before he takes a crunch** (04 LOW_BACK_*; RECHECK 01 minor). The live library holds 3 anti-movement drills plus the 2 allowed ones; a week with more core slots repeats one two or more days apart, never the day's own, and keeps it through the block. The Copenhagen plank counts as a repeat. | `core_primary.low_back_repeat_first`; `select.mjs` pickFor |
| G-78 | **Core movements have their own nouns in the near-duplicate rule.** The noun table named no core movement, so a Copenhagen plank and toes-to-bar, or a dead bug and a bird dog, read as two versions of one exercise. | `select.mjs` `CORE_NOUNS`, `anatomy` |
| G-79 | **The swimmer's presses** (04 swimming_middle; R4-LIB-03; R3-ATH-01; sample 01 major). The incline / mid-range press in slot 3 and no fixed-grip barbell press, by hot-spot code `SHOULDER_SWIMMER` only: a thrower keeps his one-arm overhead press until the coach reviews such a plan. A youth may take the flat and incline dumbbell bench presses (the library files them "advanced"). No upper-body plyo for that shoulder: the slot is a leg jump. | `incline_first_hotspot_codes`, `shoulder_press`, `youth_level_exempt_re`; `athletic.x2.upper_plyo_not_for_codes` |
| G-80 | **A third chest lift on a 3-day athletic week** (R4-SES-05 slot 3; samples 01 / 03 major: the chest floor sat on two lifts at 5 sets). A week with one B day and at most 2 pressing days takes the incline press in its "Chest / Shoulders" slot - unless the kit then leaves another pressing day without a bench press of its own. A physique plan whose chest lifts cannot hold the level's start takes it on every B day (sample 06). | `chest_shoulders_single_b`, `chest_shoulders_all_b`; `select.mjs` `chestShouldersSlots` |
| G-81 | **Loaded jumps** (EQ-15 "10-20 % of body weight"; CAT-10; samples 01 / 02). An implement heavier than 20 % of body weight is no pick (an empty hex bar is about 45 lb); no loaded jump in the Land stage or the first exercise block; a medicine ball is exempt from the stage rule. The 45 lb and the stage reading are PROPOSED. | `rules.athletic.x2.loaded_jump`; `select.mjs` hardFilter |
| G-82 | **The sprint buy-out** (OBSERVED BASEBALL Part E; sample 02 major). 1 x 20, 2 x 15, 3 x 10 calories by exercise block, all out, never over 30 calories; no buy-out on a day that ends with an aerobic session; that session takes the easy colour and the hard intervals get their own day (R2-SES-06). | `rules.conditioning.buyout_pool.x2`, `rules.conditioning.x2`; `select.mjs`, `week.mjs`, `dose.mjs` |
| G-83 | **The back keeps its sport-scaled final sets** (VOL-18; UNI-02; sample 03 major: back 19 against quads and hips at 16). It is no longer raised for the balance unless it is a Focus muscle; reverse flies and face pulls count as pulling; joint-care sets do not; while the back sits on its number for the week a press may lead the pulls by one set (`capped_slack` 1, PROPOSED) - without it a press sat at 2 sets for 12 weeks or the back arrived three weeks early. A template whose pulls at their fewest sets already hold more keeps them (HYROX). | `rules.volume.push_pull`; `volume.mjs` |
| G-84 | **A cutting sport's single-leg and one-arm work** (UNI-02, UNI-03, UNI-12; sample 03 major). The third athletic day's non-axial knee slot is free of the two-leg pin for a change-of-direction sport; with an upper target of 0.3 or less the "Chest / Shoulders" press may be the alternating incline press; `warning.laterality_capped` says what the template still holds two-sided. | `skeleton_laterality.x2_free`, `chest_slot_uni`; `select.mjs`, `validate.mjs` V30, `generate.mjs` |
| G-85 | **Class days have a skeleton** (R4-SES-05; UNI-11 / ORD-06; sample 04 majors). `class_squat`, `class_press`, `class_deadlift`, `class_pull`; the lunge runs with the clean and the pulls, the deadlift day's press is the incline press; the day's first row is a two-arm row; V30 fails a day that lost a partner its template names (a lift day whose template names none - his 3-day deadlift day - is not judged). | `amendTemplatesX2`; `rules.validation.v05.skeleton.partner`; `validate.mjs` |
| G-86 | **Hang power lifts in an L2 lifter's first block** (ATH-10; sample 04 major). The hang power clean and the hang power snatch, pulls as their swaps (PROPOSED weights). | `rules.olympic.x2.l2_block1` |
| G-87 | **The core opener stays light every week** (R2-SES-03; sample 04 major). Its effort is the opener line, never the week's reps in the tank. | `rules.core_block.x2`; `dose.mjs` |
| G-88 | **Bootcamp stations follow the kit** (OT06; R4-LIB-01; R2-LIB-08; sample 05 majors). His own slots stay; an added station the kit cannot fill gives its place to the next one. With no machine the quad station is a live single-leg knee lift and the second chest station a push-up variant (gym and field kits). A household stand-in - the table row - is no pick outside a home kit. A second back station that is a vertical pull, in a kit with no vertical pull and two rows, is a rear-delt fly and says so (`warning.no_vertical_pull`, `swap.loses.vertical_pull`). OPEN: the reviewer would hand that station to the hips; with the chest's floor of 10 that leaves 3 pulling stations against 4 presses. | `rules.session.x2.circuit_kit_aware`, `rules.selection.x2.circuit`; `week.mjs` `circuitFillable`, `select.mjs` |
| G-89 | **Circuit order** (OT06 2A; ORD-04; sample 05 major). His slots in his order are the spine; an added station is placed where it costs least, never before his own station of the same group, never an isolation exercise before a compound lift that uses its muscle. | `rules.supersets.bootcamp.x2.order`; `generate.mjs` `sequenceCircuits` |
| G-90 | **Circuit progression in timer steps** (PRG-03 "30-50 s", "3-5 rounds"; CX-02; sample 05 major: 35, 36, 37, 38 ...). 35 35 40 40 45 45 50 50 over 8 weeks and one more round in the last two weeks (PROPOSED). The plan is built again without the extra round when it would take a muscle past the absolute weekly cap (a 5-day circuit week, VOL-06). H21 holds: no drop, a number held no longer than the block's weeks spread over the steps, the block ends higher. The last week's hold sits on an isolation exercise, never on a compound standing in on an isolation station. | `rules.supersets.bootcamp.x2`; `shape.mjs`, `generate.mjs`, `dose.mjs`, `validate.mjs` |
| G-91 | **Where the sets go** (VOL-07 "first exercise first"; VOL-16; R4-SES-05; samples 01 / 06 / 07). A template exercise before the engine's extra one; a compound lift to 3 sets before an isolation of its group takes more (L2 / L3, not the athletic templates); a later exercise never above an earlier one of its group, unless the earlier one may not take a set now; no step that hands another group half a set above its number; an isolation exercise takes its 4th set in the second half of the stairway. The floor of the training days is filled before the optional day's bonus exercise takes a set. | `rules.placement.x2.order`, `iso_fourth_from_share`, `floor_first`; `volume.mjs` `addSet` |
| G-92 | **The printed schedule is the one the plan runs** (R4-PRG-01; samples 01 / 06 / 07). A block is planned up to 3 times with the start and final sets the first pass found. | G-93 | **Extra exercises** (R4-SES-05 "an extra small muscle exercise at the end"; VOL-17; R3-SES-01; R4-PRG-04). The template's own rear-delt station opens first (direct rear-delt sets 0 / 2 / 4 by level, PROPOSED); the optional day's bonus goes to the group missing the largest share; an extra exercise opens for a start the default sets cannot hold, never for a group whose optional-day stations already hold all but one exercise of its ceiling (one set of the ceiling stays free for helper credit, PROPOSED). | `rules.placement.x2.template_extra`, `bonus_order`, `extra_for_start`, `extra_ceiling_margin` |
| G-94 | **Pain words** (VOL-03; R2-LIB-03; sample 08 major). The pain line is said up front; "chosen to protect your knee" only under a lift the pain changed, with the lift it replaced; a knee lift carries the pain-free-depth cue. A swap is never a placeholder record; a beginner's swap on a slot the pain touches never loads the spine heavily. The plain pull-up is a library exercise, not a placeholder. | `rules.volume.pain.x2`, `rules.selection.x2.pain_swap`, `swaps`; `select.mjs`, `dose.mjs`, `generate.mjs` |
| G-95 | **Swap notes** (R4-LIB-04). New "loses" lines: the vertical pull, the isolation, the core action, the external load. | `library.mjs` `losesKey` |
| G-96 | **Benchmark scorer 2.3** (`design/bench/score.mjs`; listed in its header). Readings that follow rules of this round: a high-incline press is a press; the youth exemption; the template's own extra station is no misplaced bonus; a carry as a pick counts under add-on picks (stricter); the staircase reads a compound lift at no more than the 3 sets it takes first; the circuit rule of G-90; the free knee slot of G-84. Three gaps of 2.2 in "the closest share the dial could reach": a heavy-powerlifting profile's presses and the incline-first slot are held two-arm by the engine's own rules, and the count of one-arm lifts is read on the client's kit. Those three move 16 plans up without a change to the plans (see `samples/STATUS_v0.5.md`). | `design/bench/score.mjs` |

### Engine 0.6, work package "words": what the plan prints (2026-10-11)

Source: the round-4 coach-eye reviews of the 8 samples (`reviews/coach-eye-r4/0*.md`), the items about printed words in English and
Spanish. Layer: `tools/fixture/v06-words.mjs` (after `v05-fix2.mjs`, in both builds); the engine reads it as `rules.render.v6` and
`rules.volume.pain.x6`. No exercise pick, set count or effort rule changes: only what the plan says about them (and two plan notes in
`dose.mjs`). Every new line is PROPOSED wording; the machine-written Spanish is listed in `model/i18n/es_review.json > priority_review`.
Tests: `tests/v06-words.test.mjs` (22 tests, named by coach answer and review id).

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-160 | **Circuit progression is told from the weeks** (R4-PRG-03 the rule the person applies; R4-PRG-02; CX-02; review 05 major 2: "the rounds stay ... climbs week by week" over a plan that adds a 5th round in week 7 and raises the time every 2nd week). "How you progress" and the weekly-table note read the rounds and work seconds of the first circuit training block: "4 rounds, then 5 from week 7; the work time per station goes from 35 s to 50 s, a step every 2 weeks". "The rounds stay at N" prints only when no week changes them. A week that adds a round says so under "What changes" ("One more round: 5 rounds a station (was 4)"). | `explain.mjs` `circuitStory`, `changesText`; `render.mjs` `progressSection`, `weeklySetsSection` |
| G-161 | **A circuit station's dose in the block table reads from its first week to its last** ("4 rounds x 35 s → 5 rounds x 50 s"), not from the first week of each exercise block (it read "→ 4 rounds x 45 s"). | `render.mjs` `blockView` |
| G-162 | **The pyramid is written out** (COACH-ANSWER PRG-07 "12, 10, 8, and then 20 ... You drop back to the 20 for a light-weight, high-rep set"; review 06 major 3). "5 sets: 12, 10, 8, 8, then a light back-off set of 20" and his note under it (mid weight, heavier as the reps drop, heaviest on the last heavy set, then light). The single-range rep note (`note.rep_cap`, "Keep this lift at 8-10 reps") is dropped from an item with per-set reps in `dose.mjs`, and never printed under one. No percentage is invented for the drop: his sheet says "light". | `dose.mjs` resistanceDose; `render.mjs` `formatSetReps`, `itemDose`; `rules.render.v6.pyramid` |
| G-163 | **The pain-free depth cue follows the exercise type** (VOL-03; R3-ATH-02; review 08 major 3: "set the stops or use a box" under a step-up and a leg extension). First match wins: a step-up uses a low step of about 6-12 in / 15-30 cm (the athletic rules' low box), a machine sets its range (seat, pad or stops), a two-leg squat sits to a box, a lunge shortens its range. The step height is written in the cue whatever box the intake names. PROPOSED words and step height. | `dose.mjs` `depthNoteKey`; `rules.volume.pain.x6` |
| G-164 | **"What changes" says the real set change of each exercise** (R4-PRG-03; review 04 major 2: a fly went 2 → 4 and the line said "+1 set"). A set line is said once per exercise with its sets the week before and this week ("+2 sets on DB Floor Press (Saturday): 2 → 4 sets"). A slot new this week, or next to a test week, keeps the plain line. | `explain.mjs` `changesText` |
| G-165 | **A new exercise block names what changes, day by day** (review 07 minor 8: "11 exercises change a little" named none): one line per day, "Tuesday: Single-Arm DB/KB Row → Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip)". | `explain.mjs` `blockChangeLines` |
| G-166 | **The top of the week-level scale is "your hardest week"** (GEN-08 / H02 "never to failure"; reviews 02 m9, 06 m11, 08 m9: "11 = a little past your limits"). Words only; the effort rules and the stair levels do not change. | `plan.stair_level` |
| G-167 | **"Trained through your sport work" only with sport practice** (reviews 02 m14, 08 m4). Adding an arm slot is a template question for the template work package, not this one. | `render.mjs` `warningKey` |

| G-169 | **The pain line promises a note "on the lift" only when a lift carries one** (review 08 m2; fix round 2 G-94 prints that note only under a lift the pain changed). | `render.mjs` `warningKey`; `warning.pain_plan*_no_lift` |
| G-170 | **A class member reads his own WOD line** (review 04 m11; OBSERVED 'COMPETE CROSSFIT SPLIT' A3:A7 is competitor copy; CX-04). In the CrossFit-style family a client who does not compete reads "The WODs in this plan are your conditioning on these days: do them in place of your box's WOD, not on top of it" instead of "WODs will be prescribed by your box". His other lines stay verbatim. PROPOSED wording for the coach. | `rules.render.v6.class_member_instruction`; `render.mjs` `instructionBlock` |
| G-171 | **Small layout words** (review 03 m12): the block view heading reads "Training block, week by week · Week 1-8" when the block's own label already says "block" (it read "Block · Training block", and plain "Training block" would repeat the stairway heading); a one-leg jump says "contacts per leg". | `render.mjs` `blockView`, `itemDose` |

Groups with the same step share one line ("25 % more sets for Quads, Back and Chest"); a circuit week that added a round and moved every group reads "for every muscle group: one more circuit round". ### Engine 0.6, volume targets and set placement (2026-10-11)

Source: the round-4 coach-eye reviews of the 8 samples (`reviews/coach-eye-r4/0*.md`), the volume majors: 01 / 02 / 03 M2 (main
lift first), 04 / 06 / 08 / 01 M2 (the optional day's bonus), 07 M1 (biceps capped in silence), 06 M1 (a physique plan that starts
at its top), 04 M1 (the class member's tops), 03 M2 (an upper body at maintenance), 05 M3 (a circuit above its top), 04 M2 (one
step an exercise; the change lines). Authority: round 4 + CT-01 > CX > round 3 > round 2 > full > chat > sheets. Model layer:
`tools/fixture/v06-volume.mjs` (after `v05-fix2.mjs`, in both builds); the engine reads it as `rules.placement.x3` and
`rules.volume.x3`, every key with a guard. Items marked PROPOSED are the engine's own numbers or readings where the coach gave none.
The engine version is not bumped here (three parallel packages merge into 0.6; the lead bumps it once).

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-100 | **Main lift first** (VOL-07 'add it to the first exercise on the list ... 3, 3, 3 ... 4, 3, 3'; R3-VOL-03; reviews 01 / 02 / 03 M2). Among a group's exercises at its fewest sets, the training day whose first lift of the group is the template's main two-leg lift (`work` slot) takes the next set first, on the exercise of that day that holds the fewest sets, so that day climbs the coach's own staircase and its main lift leads it - at most `max_ahead` (2, PROPOSED) sets above the group's least-loaded exercise of another day. Inside a day the first exercise still leads (session order); the two exposures of one day type keep alternating (R3-VOL-03). Read with the scorer's staircase (spread at most 1 inside a day), the main lift is never more than one set above its day's other exercises - it climbs as soon as they do. Samples: 01 front squat 3 -> 4 from week 7 (was 3 for 9 weeks); 03 front squat 3 -> 4 from week 4 (was 3 flat). | `placement.x3.lead_first`; `volume.mjs` addSet `kWork` |
| G-101 | **An athlete's flat press holds at most 4 sets** (reviews 02 / 03 M2: a flat dumbbell bench at 4-5 sets while the main leg lift held 2-3; PROPOSED number) - only while the group keeps 3 exercises of its own in the week (a pitcher's one chest press is not held under the floor). The chest is held at what that gives and the plan says so (01: chest 13 of 14). | `placement.x3.athlete_press_max`; `volume.mjs` boundsOf |
| G-102 | **The optional day's bonus exercise is for small groups only** (R3-VOL-03 'extra SMALL-muscle sets become a short bonus exercise on the optional day'; ORD-10; R4-SES-05 'an extra small muscle exercise at the end'; reviews 04 / 06 M2). A big group (quads, hips, back, chest) takes no bonus exercise on the optional day and no extra exercise at the end of a training day; its final sets are held on the training days, else it is capped and said (G-107). The class family's second knee lift is the one named exception (G-105). 06: back and chest end at 20 of 25 on the training days (the optional-day row and fly are gone); 04: the WOD day's floor press is gone. The 'Chest / Shoulders' capacity rule of selection (G-80 / fix round 2) no longer counts a chest bonus, so 06 keeps an incline press on both B days. | `placement.x3.bonus_small_only`; `volume.mjs` bonus loop, `openExtra`; `select.mjs` chest_shoulders_all_b |
| G-103 | **A big group's optional-day Focus slots close** when it is not the person's own Focus (R3-VOL-03, ORD-10; review 01 M2: a sport-priority split squat the day after Friday's and an extra seated row). `always: true` (PROPOSED): the slots close whatever the training days hold; a group they cannot take to its final sets is capped and said (01: back 20 of 21). Recorded in `meta.stairway[].focus_closed`. | `placement.x3.big_optional_focus_close`; `volume.mjs` planStairs |
| G-104 | **A bonus exercise never repeats a compound pattern the day before trained** (review 01 M2; PER-04): read on the exercise's library pattern (squat, hinge, lunge, single-leg hinge, rows, presses, pull-downs); an optional day whose previous day trained it is skipped. | `placement.x3.bonus_no_repeat_previous_day`; `volume.mjs` openBonus |
| G-105 | **With no optional day for it, a small group's bonus exercise goes on a training day** (R3-VOL-03 'with no optional day, on a day that already trains biceps, never as the only biceps exercise of that day'; R4-SES-02 the day's size; review 07 M1): the day that trains the group with the fewest exercises, inside the session size, part of the block from week 1 (`why.bonus_main_day`). 07: a third curl on Tuesday, biceps 15.5 -> 25.75 (was capped at 22 of 25 with no notice). The class family's squat day takes a second knee lift the same way (review 04 M1 hint, PROPOSED): 04 quads 12 -> 18 (was 15 of 18). | `placement.x3.bonus_main_day_fallback`; `volume.mjs` bonus loop |
| G-106 | **2 days: the training days hold the band first** (R4-SES-03 'every big muscle group ... about 6-8'; review 08 M2): the training days hold the band's low end (6) for every trained group before the optional day adds, and a small group's bonus exercise goes to a training day that already trains it when the day has room. 08: the shoulders hold 6.25 -> 7 on Monday and Thursday every week (was 4.25 -> 6). | `placement.x3.two_day_main_floor`; `volume.mjs` floorMain, bonus loop |
| G-107 | **Every capped group is said** (CX-04; H10 / H17 'a group the template cannot take that far is held at what the slots hold, and the plan says so'; review 07 M1): `capped[].notice` is `true` for every capped group, its line is printed up front (`warning.capped_by_template`) and it is a summary shortfall of the block's last week. Retires the 80 % notice threshold of G-64 (PROPOSED there). | `placement.x3.capped_notice_all`, `capped_shortfalls`; `volume.mjs` runBlock |

PROPOSED number. 06: back 16 -> 20, chest 16 -> 20, quads 16 -> 25 (they started at 19-20 with 5-set first lifts); week 1 holds 109 sets (was 121). A 6-day push / pull / legs week keeps the level's start. | `volume.x3.nonfocus_start`; `volume.mjs` targetsFor |

### Engine 0.6 selection package: selection, templates and week layout (2026-10-11)

Source: the round-4 coach-eye reviews of the 8 samples (`reviews/coach-eye-r4/`, majors 01 M1, 02 M1, 02 M3, 03 M1, 03 M3, 05 M1,
07 M2, 08 M1). Every fix is in the engine or its data, never in a plan. Model layer: `tools/fixture/v06-select.mjs` (after
`v05-fix2.mjs`, in both builds); the engine reads it as `rules.selection.s6`, `rules.conditioning.s6`, the arm station of the athletic
A / B day types and one more movement family (`rules.selection.r2.families` `seal_row`). Tests: `tests/v06-select.test.mjs`. Items
marked PROPOSED are the engine's own numbers or readings where the coach gave none.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-130 | **The athletic A / B days hold slot 5** (COACH-R4 R4-SES-05 "athletic days: the athletic block, then the same A / B lifts" - A: biceps, B: triceps; 01 M1, 02 M1: no arm exercise for a swimmer whose profile lists triceps as a priority, nor for a linebacker whose profile asks for direct triceps sets, on days that held 9-11 of the 12). Every athletic A / B day type (2, 3, 4 days and the same-pattern variant) takes an `ISO_BICEP` (A) or `ISO_TRICEP` (B) station, printed in slot 5's place (before the leg extension / leg curl) and ranked last, so it is the first thing the person may remove (R4-SES-04). It holds the template's isolation dose (2 sets); the arms stay credit-only in the stairway for a field athlete (COACH-R3 R3-VOL-01 "arms stay helper work"), so `credit_only_groups` still names them and no floor of 10 is asked of them. **Difference from his athletic sheet (OBSERVED BYOP 'ATHLETIC - FULL BODY - 4 DAYS A', `analysis/programs/prog_byop_athletic.md` point 7):** his sheet drops 'Bicep' and 'Tricep' from the AESTHETICS skeleton and caps the day at 10; round 4 is newer and names slot 5 on the athletic days, so round 4 wins. The station is kept for a competitive athlete whose sport profile writes direct arm sets (04 weekly_sets `ISO_BICEP` / `ISO_TRICEP` > 0: 32 of 127 profiles, the swimmer and the linebacker among them; condition `if_athletic_arm_sets`); the other field sports (the soccer reviewer, 03 m14, asked to keep his sheet), the stay-active adult (5-10 exercises, G-68) and the in-season variant are kept as written (PROPOSED, open for the coach). An arm the main days train in slot 5 takes no optional-day Focus slot for the profile's direct sets (benchmark 27: three triceps Focus slots on top of it passed the triceps ceiling). | `amendTemplatesS6`, `week.keepStation` (condition `if_athletic_arm_sets`), `week.planFocus` |
| G-131 | **Low-back core only with low-back pain** (02 M3: a healthy linebacker got planks, side planks, dead bugs, bird dogs and Copenhagen planks for 12 weeks because his profile's injury list names `LOW_BACK_LOADING`). The low-back list (E-129 / G-76 / G-77: anti-movement drills only, the plank repeat) applies when the intake's pain list holds `lower_back`, and (PROPOSED, the conservative reading) for a client from the 50+ overlay whose profile has the hot spot - the v0.4 review of the 52-year-old court player asked for it (`hotspot_list_from_age` 50). A profile hot spot alone, under 50, is a lean, not a list: anti-movement drills +2, loaded or long-lever trunk flexion -2, weighted / decline sit-ups -6 more; the dead bug and the bird dog stay pickable; the profile's core plane shares (`core_rules.plane_pct`, E-184) then deal rotational and anti-rotation drills to the main days. A heavy athlete (220 lb+) keeps his hanging and long-lever holds as a fallback only (`rules.selection.r2.core.heavy_avoid_re`, the 02 minor of v0.4, which the list used to hold). **Core block changes** (PER-02; 02 M3 "only the hold times move"): one core exercise of every main day changes at an exercise-block boundary, inside its plane and kind (the E-186 rule), and stays when the live library holds no such exercise the week does not use. PROPOSED weights. | `rules.selection.s6.core`; `select.lowBackClient`, `f3Score`, `pickFor` (`lbHotCore`), `selectBlock` |
| G-132 | **Single-leg and lateral jumps from the first block when the plan starts at stage 2** (R2-ATH-03 "an intermediate athlete may start at stage 2"; CAT-13; 03 M3: a soccer midfielder - 60 % single-leg, 30 % lateral profile - had two-foot jumps only, because the first exercise block switched the single-leg option off, an engine inference). A training block whose jump work starts at RC2 or RC3 (no Land block before it) takes single-leg reactive and ballistic jumps from its first exercise block; the two-foot rule of the first reactive block (E-32) stays for depth jumps and drops. "Starts at stage 2" is read on the whole plan: no exercise block before it, in any training block, is a Land block (a baseball pre-season after a Land off-season keeps the two-foot first reactive block; without this the sweep found its jumps over the V27 budget). A cutting field / court sport (`family: field_court`, `cod_sport`) holds at least one single-leg or lateral jump station on every training day (+8, PROPOSED); in the Land stage (RC1) that station is a two-foot lateral jump, single-leg from the Land & Go stage on (the Land stage lands on two feet first, CAT-10 - benchmark 38, tennis, lost jump contacts to single-leg drops). A lateral jump is read on the id (`lateral`, `side`, `skater`, `cossack`, `zig-zag` as a word: `select.LATERAL_JUMP_RE`; the generic direction reader matched 'bi*lateral*'). A plan that starts at RC1 still lands on two feet first. | `rules.selection.s6.jumps`; `select.prep` (`jumpStart2`, `cuttingDaily`), `hardFilter`, `scoreOf`, `record` |
| G-133 | **The safe power slot** (EQ-03 "old, injured or a lot of high volume: collect that volume in a safe place with more constraints"; ATH-10 beginners DB / KB, hex-bar jumps; 04 rec_pickleball `triple_ext.level_note` "no snatch variants"; 08 M1: a 52-year-old beginner with knee pain who plays 3 times a week got a single-arm DB snatch and a barbell mid-hang clean pull). With declared pain, 3 or more play days a week (PROPOSED threshold: R3-ATH-05's heavy play week) or the 50+ racket overlay, the snatch family leaves the power slot (the DB / KB high pull stays); a snatch-day station takes a KB swing or the high pull, and a clean-day station that no clean or loaded jump can fill for the client takes them before an overhead jerk; a beginner (L1) with pain gets no barbell power lift (hard filter; the `x.olympic_split_grip` weight is no longer the only guard). V19 does not read the day pattern on such a relaxed pick (item flag `olympic_safe`): a KB swing on the knee day of a client whose knee rules out the KB squat clean and whose body weight rules out the 45-lb hex bar (EQ-15) is the safe choice, not a pattern error. | `rules.selection.s6.olympic_safe`; `select.variantPool`, `hardFilter`, `pickFor`; `validate.v19Olympic` |
| G-134 | **One-detail block changes** (R2-LIB-01, PER-02, PER-04; 07 M2: a one-arm DB row became a two-arm chest-supported seal row - two seal rows that week -, a one-arm pulldown a rope pulldown, a split squat a deficit reverse lunge). A block change is the same exercise with one concrete detail, or a new exercise that keeps the old one's laterality and support (free-standing, chest-supported, machine; read from the record id, PROPOSED) and differs in one detail - a static split squat and a stepping lunge count as two. On the coach's gym, push / pull / legs and class days the upper-body dial keeps each station's laterality from the previous exercise block (the dial still sets block 1; the athletic days keep the dial, UNI-02 / UNI-03). The seal rows are one movement family, once a week (E-182). | `rules.selection.s6.block_change`, `rules.selection.r2.families`; `select.decideUpperLaterality`, `pickFor` (forceChange) |
| G-135 | **The bootcamp's cardio station is cardio** (OBSERVED BYOP 'BOOTCAMP - OPTION 2' Cardio in 1A / 2A / 3A; PRG-03; 05 M1: a KB swing on Monday and a medball slam on Wednesday dosed as max-intent power and sorted to station 1). The `COND_CIRCUIT` station of the `fat_loss_bootcamp` family takes a cardio pick the kit holds (jump rope, jumping jacks, burpees, high knees ...) before a ballistic one (-14, PROPOSED); a cardio pick is dosed as continuous work for the station (`effort.circuit_metabolic`, unchanged in dose.mjs) and keeps the coach's station order (only a ballistic station leads the circuit, G-89). The B2 circuit-ballistic rule (`rules.athletic.circuit_ballistic`, INFERRED) is off for the listed family (`athletic.circuitBallistic`): the general-population bootcamp holds no ballistic station and no throw budget, which also removes the "athletic work: 20 throws" line the 05 reviewer called jargon (m13). ### Engine 0.6.0 integration: the volume, selection and words packages merged (2026-10-11)

The three packages were built in parallel and are applied in this order in both builds: ... `v05-fix2.mjs`, `v06-volume.mjs`,
`v06-select.mjs` (rules after), `v06-words.mjs`; word tables `UI_V06`, `UI_S6`, `UI_W6` (a later table wins). `ENGINE_VERSION` is
`0.6.0`. Benchmark: `design/bench/results_v06.json` + `V06.md` is the reference from here on (`run.mjs` default label `v06`);
`results_v05.json` + `V05.md` stay unchanged as the engine 0.5.0 history. The side runs of the three packages (`results_v06vol`,
`results_v06sel`, `results_v06words` and their `.md`) were removed: no decision cites them and the merged run supersedes them.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-190 | **One change line per exercise, with its real sets before and after** (root cause of the v06-volume "words in English and Spanish" and "CX-02 '+n sets'" failures and of the words package's "+2 with from -> to" failure). The volume package records one change per exercise with its planned step (`changes.set_added`, or `changes.sets_added_n` with `n`, G-109) and gave `changes.sets_added_n` the words '+{n} sets on {exercise} ({day})'; the words package rewrote the same key as '... : {from} → {to} sets' (G-164) but only turned `changes.set_added` into it, so a `sets_added_n` record printed raw `{from}` / `{to}` and `UI_W6` silently overrode `UI_V06`. Now: `UI_V06` carries the words package's wording, `changes.sets_added_n_plain` keeps the plain line, the volume record carries `from` / `to` (planned), and `explain.changesText` reads both keys: the line names the item's printed sets the week before and this week; next to a test week, where the test takes the place of the tested lift's top set and the printed sets differ from the planned step, the plain line with the planned step is kept (G-164 "next to a test week keeps the plain line" - the words package read it on an item flag that is never set). | `engine/explain.mjs` `changesText`; `engine/volume.mjs` (the week's change list); `tools/fixture/v06-volume.mjs` `UI_V06` |
| G-191 | **One acceleration line per week** (R4-PRG-02 "a clear note"; review 05 m10). The words package's `accelLines` printed one line per step size, so a week whose groups jumped by different percentages still printed several lines. Now one line names every fast group, with the step range when the steps differ ("20-25 % more sets for Quads and Hips"); the circuit-round wording when one extra round moved every group by the same step. | `engine/render.mjs` `accelLines` |

| G-197 | **CH5-01 team practices are the athlete's own to account for** (COACH-CH5 CH5-01, `coach_answers_chat_2026-10-11.md`: "team practice doesnt matter, just have the athlete take into account and they handle that"; newest authority on this subject). The G-136 reading "a competitive athlete's practices count as aerobic sessions and show as practice days" is retired: `rules.conditioning.s6.practice.counts_as_aerobic` is `false`, so `intake.practiceCount` gives 0, no practice day is placed in the week and no aerobic session is replaced - the template's aerobic sessions stay. A competitive athlete with practice days (`meta.season.practice_days > 0`) reads one line up front (EN + ES, PROPOSED wording): "This plan does not count your 2 team practices a week: take them into account and adjust the load yourself ...". The hard-eve rule of G-136 (no Blue intervals the day before sprints or the heavy hinge) stays. | `tools/fixture/v06-select.mjs` (`conditioning.s6.practice`); `tools/fixture/v06-words.mjs` (`render.v6.team_practice_line`, `ui.plan.team_practice`); `render.mjs` `noticesSection` |

The G-130 limit to sports whose profile writes direct arm sets, and the in-season exception, are retired (`rules.selection.s6.arm_slot.every_athletic_day`): biceps on the A day, triceps on the B day, every sport, season and stay-active plan. The station stays the day's last rank (the first thing the person may remove, R4-SES-04) and is never left out by the day's exercise count: with room it takes its place (a place an athletic station the day left out may still take back with the athletic budget is kept for it, as is a place heavy play's cuts leave for the throw); on a full day it takes the place of the day's last-ranked isolation accessory - never an athletic, power, core or main lift - and the day says so (`why.arm_slot`, `full_day: 'replace_last_accessory'`, PROPOSED), a stated reason V30 reads (`validation.v05.skeleton.absent_ok_whys`), so the day stays inside its client type's range (R4-SES-02, V13). A step on a big group's bonus exercise leaves the A / B rotation of the template lifts where it was (R3-VOL-03). `volume.x3.group_max.sets` 35: no group is planned above 35 weekly sets. **Benchmark scorer 2.4.1**: the two exposures of a day type are read on the day type's own lifts, not on a bonus exercise the engine adds to one training day. Read per candidate (a transitive order): the `work` lift whose twin (the same day type's other exposure, also in line for the set) holds more sets goes first, before the Focus bias, while the step keeps its day's staircase (at most one set above another compound lift of its group that day). 06: hex-bar deadlift 3 → 4 (week 10), hip thrust 3 → 4 → 5 (weeks 6, 11); 01 / 02 / 03 unchanged. | `placement.x3.work_lead.twins_alternate`; `volume.mjs` addSet `kTwinGo` |

The face pull has no set left to climb (L1 holds 4 an exercise): it progresses by weight and reps (CX-02). Never above the plan's own top (R4-PRG-04, read as the ceiling check reads it: direct sets at most the top, credited sets at most one set above it): when the training days plus the bonus would pass it, the training days carry the group alone, with no bonus exercise; when a first run of the block still passes it (helper credit that climbs with the other groups' steps), the block is planned again with the group's final sets one lower, so no own step is asked on top of that credit (`hints.two_day_top`; benchmark 71, a 72-year-old golfer on 2 days with a bodyweight kit: shoulders 8 from week 1, 8.25-8.75 credited, instead of a week-12 pike push-up step to 9.75). | `volume.mjs` `floorMain`, `twoDayAlso`, `boundsOf`, `planStairs` |

### Engine 0.6.0 fix round on the round-7 coach-eye reviews (2026-10-12)

Source: `reviews/coach-eye-r7/02-football.md` (M1, m16-m18) and `08-pickleball-genpop.md` (M1, m3, m4). The last two fix rounds
oscillated (G-207 made 08's Friday fly "extra"; G-209 gave 02's chest its 13th set with a Tuesday fly from week 1) because both majors
have one root cause: a bonus exercise was opened, or took a set, while its group's template lifts still had room, or when the training
days already held the group's band. This round replaces the readings with ONE rule for every bonus exercise of a group,
`rules.placement.x3.bonus_last` (`tools/fixture/v06-volume.mjs`, both builds; every key read with a guard). Authority: CH5 + round 4 +
CT-01 > CX > round 3 > round 2 > full > chat > sheets. Tests: `tests/v06-fix3.test.mjs` (named VOL-07, CH5-03, R3-VOL-03, R4-SES-03).
The engine stays 0.6.0 (a fix round inside the 0.6.0 release; the benchmark reference keeps the label `v06`).

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-217 | **A bonus exercise opens only in the exercise block that needs it** (VOL-07 'if there are 3 exercises and I need to add an extra set of volume, then I add it to the first exercise on the list'; CH5-03 'they should reach more IF NEEDED'; review r7 02 M1: a Tuesday fly from week 1 for a 13th chest set the presses could not hold only in the last block). Every bonus exercise of a group - the optional day's and a training day's (G-102 / G-105 / G-199) - is one rule; the template's own extra station (R4-SES-05, `why.template_extra_station`) and a muscle's direct-minimum exercise (R2-VOL-04, now `why.muscle_direct_exercise`) are template work and keep their place (`bonus_last.template_whys`). `open_when_needed`: the opening is read on the block's schedule, exercise block by exercise block: a bonus opens with the first block in which some week is more than the group's template lifts - every one at its own top that week, `athlete_press_max` and `later_capped` included - and the bonuses already open can hold, and only when, at that block's last week, every template lift at its top plus the fewest sets of its open bonuses stays inside the block's ceiling (the final sets; one under them before the last two weeks, R4-PRG-01). That last week may be raised by one set to fit (PROPOSED); a bigger jump, or an opening that would take the group to its final sets before the last two weeks from its start, waits for a later block or never opens. A training day's bonus that a later block needs waits at 0 sets until then; an optional day's opens with its block as before. The weeks before it opens are what the group's lifts hold without it (for that group only - the joint fit of every group's steps still reads the caps with every bonus open, so no other group's steps move), and the week it opens leaves room for its fewest sets (the week before holds that much less, never under the group's start). A bonus the first week's start or floor needs opens with the first block whatever the template's lifts then hold (H17, the floor is the higher rule; bench 56's elbow-pain triceps). `meta.stairway[].main_bonus` lists a training day's bonuses with the exercise block each opens with. | `volume.mjs` planStairs (`gb`, `gbOpen`, `gCap`, `weekMin`, `dormant`), the week loop; `generate.mjs` meta.stairway |
| G-218 | **A bonus never takes a set while a template lift of its group has room** (VOL-07; CH5-03; review r7 02 M1: the fly took chest's week-9 set while the bench sat at 3 under its cap of 4). `template_first`: in `addSet` a group's bonus is no candidate while any template lift of the group, on any day, can still take a set under its own top that week; the day's first lift leads (G-209's `kMainBig` reading, 'a bonus under every template lift takes its turn as the fewest', is retired). For a group that holds a bonus, a press whose next set would put the week's pulling under `press_hold.min_pull_per_push` (0.8) of its pressing is at its top (VOL-18 'balance this out'; the scorer's T8-BAL-03) - the bonus, not a press, takes that set (bench 01: the pec-deck fly from week 1, as before). A bonus does not give back a set it took when a template lift's top later opens (an isolation's 4th set in the hardest weeks): the scorer's per-exercise 'never down' (T3-STW-01) is the higher reading, and the next set goes to the template lift. | `volume.mjs` addSet |
| G-219 | **A whole bonus or none: CH5-03 read for a shortfall smaller than a bonus exercise** (CH5-03 'reach more if needed'; R4-PRG-04 'never above the top'; H10 'a group the template cannot take that far is held at what the slots hold, and the plan says so'; review r7 02 M1 'either hold chest at 12 of 13, marked capped and said, or add a 2-set bonus at the top only - the coach's call'). `whole_bonus_only`: a group keeps a bonus only while its template lifts at their tops plus every bonus's fewest sets stay inside its final sets. 02's chest holds 12 with every press at 4 (bench, incline, DB bench); a fly of 2 sets takes it to 14, above its final 13 and its L3 top. The late-block alternative was built and measured: to arrive at 13 no earlier than week 11 with a 2-set fly opening in week 9, the presses must hold chest at 10 for weeks 1-8 (its floor), then +2 (20 %) in week 9 - a flat chest for 8 weeks and a jump, the opposite of the stairway (R3-VOL-04) - or a press must stay under its cap next to the fly, which is the M1 pattern again. So the bonus is not "needed" in CH5-03's sense (the presses reach the most the template allows) and chest is held at 12 of 13 and said: "Chest: this template holds 12 weekly sets, below the planned 13. Add an exercise if you want more." The same rule holds 01's back at 20 of 21 (four rows at 5; a 2-set row would make 22): its Monday cable row from week 1 is gone and the plan says so (its 0.75 helper credit to the shoulders goes with it: shoulders 13 -> 12). **Open for the coach:** for a one-set shortfall, should the slot-1 press take a 5th set (L3 top 5; review r6 02 hint b), or the plan stay held and said? | `volume.mjs` planStairs (`whole_bonus_only`) |
| G-220 | **The first exposure of a day type steps first** (R3-VOL-03 'alternate between the two exposures - Monday's first back exercise, then Thursday's'; VOL-07; review r7 02 M1: Friday's DB bench took chest's week-11 set before Tuesday's bench). `first_exposure_first`: between two candidates of a group on the two exposures of one day type that hold the same sets, the day whose template lifts of the group hold fewer sets on average goes first (the exposures alternate), and at the same average the first exposure goes first - instead of the rotation from the day used last. 02: Tuesday's bench 3 -> 4 in week 2, Friday's DB bench in week 5, the incline in week 11. | `volume.mjs` addSet (`kExp`) |
| G-221 | **2 days: a group the training days hold inside the band takes no bonus** (R4-SES-03 'about 6-8 ... never pile on'; CH5-03 'if needed'; review r7 08 M1: Monday + Thursday held the shoulders at 6.25 -> 7 and the Friday rear-delt fly still came on top - 8 -> 9, above the shoulders' cap of 8 and above every big group, two rear-delt lifts in 24 hours). `two_day_no_extra`: G-207's "the optional day's bonus is extra" is retired; such a group's final sets are what the training days hold. 08: shoulders 6.25 -> 7 (final 7, was 9), no Friday fly; the face pull keeps 4 sets on Thursday. | `volume.mjs` planStairs (T2a) |
| G-222 | **A bonus prints last, says why, and the plan says how many final sets sit on it** (VOL-07 the bonus is the extra; R4-SES-04 the easiest to remove; H10 'said plainly'; reviews r7 02 m16 / m18, 08 m3). `print_last`: a group's bonus is the last lift of its day (the template's own added stations keep E-87's place before the isolations); its note is `note.bonus_final` ("Bonus exercise, done last: your template's lifts for this muscle group are already at their top sets, so this one adds the last sets to reach your final number."); one line up front per group whose final sets sit partly on a training day's bonus (`ui.plan.bonus_line_main`, `_n`), with the week it comes in (`ui.plan.bonus_from_week`), G-204 style: "Chest: 2 of its 14 final sets sit on a bonus exercise, done last in its day (Flat DB Fly, Friday, from week 9), because your template's lifts for it are at their top sets. Skip it and the chest total ends at about 12." The optional-day lines name the week too, and say "the {group} total ends" / "el total de {grupo} queda" (08 m3: 'shoulders ends', 'hombros termina'). EN + ES, PROPOSED words. | `generate.mjs` withAddedSlots; `dose.mjs`; `render.mjs` optionalBonusLines; `tools/fixture/v06-volume.mjs`, `v06-words.mjs` |
| G-223 | **Benchmark scorer 2.5** (a rule changed on purpose). A group's bonus exercise (`rules.placement.x3.bonus_last`; the item's first why outside `template_whys`) is left out of the day's staircase - it opens only with every template lift of its group at its top, so it sits at its fewest sets next to them (the 4 / 4 / 2 day the G-209 reading avoided) - and is read on its own rule: a week it takes a set while a template lift of its group, on any day, is under its top (the press cap with `later_capped`, an optional-day Focus slot's top, a tested lift held in its test week) counts as a staircase deviation. The opportunity count is unchanged. Without the rule the scorer reads as 2.4.1. | `design/bench/score.mjs` |
| G-224 | **Not done in this round** (left for later rounds, unchanged): 02 m1 (open, G-205), m2-m15 (m15 'easiest to remove' labels), m17 is answered here (G-209's second half and G-215 are superseded by G-218 / G-220); 08 m1 (the face pull's 4 sets from week 1: it holds the shoulders' band on the training days, G-207 first half, kept), m2, m5-m19 (Spanish slips other than m3). | - |
